- **E-21. If a player is DOWNED during a TELL**: the tell continues; their noise stops (a downed seat pushes nothing); their beam is whatever the Emitter State reports; a DOWNED seat counts as living for the baseline ring and the pod hold (AT-31). The tell is world-level, never player-level.
- **E-22. If every living player dies during a TELL**: the death stub restores the newest verified autosave (SL-7) and Attention rolls back with it (WORLD scope), including `rng_counter`, `tell_len` and every timer, so a tell already drawn in the snapshot replays its length and beats exactly (no reroll), while every tell drawn after the restore takes the new `restore_epoch` key and so does not replay the length the dead run met (user ruling D16, AT-8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **E-23. If a player hops by pod during a TELL** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(creative-director ruling on OQ-AT-12; **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions)**, including that a home-ship-locus tell is not voidable by hopping)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: the tell clock continues, the bed and beats are unchanged and `held_ring` keeps `B` until arrival (a departure never aborts the tell early). If the locus body is not the home-ship body and every seat recorded in it at TELL entry has left it by the due run (`locus_vacated`), the due-run decision is an **abort**, not a breach: TELL → CALM, `tell_outcome = ABORTED`, no STRESS, no beacon, `rearm_until_T` set, the release cue plays. The departed derelict going dormant (WM-24) does not trigger the AT-14 fallback, because the void is resolved first; the breach is never moved onto the home ship the player escaped to. If a living seat stays in the locus body, the breach proceeds on that body. A home-ship locus is not voidable (E-27 and the Moment B variant: no pod escape from a home-ship breach); Web Map's ASK-A4 (hold the transfer while no seat is aboard home) is declined and the home-locus breach proceeds as ruled, and the case with nobody aboard the home ship is E-73 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Only if the locus held no living seat at TELL entry and Web Map keeps it loaded (a berthed derelict) does the breach proceed on the recorded `locus_body`, ending under X10 at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` (F-17). X10 is reachable at MVP only through that berthed case; the departed-derelict form is fixture-only.
- **E-73. If the locus is the home ship and no living seat is aboard the home body at TELL entry, at BREACH entry, or at both** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* (reachable without contrivance: residual noise at home, a weld or a run, followed by a sortie before the home ledger has decayed; a hop that voided a tell (a void leaves the level unrelieved, `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` 1000, AT-10) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; or the quiet-crew case of Moment C, where the lit home ship's ledger out-weighs a still player elsewhere): `tell_occupants_at_entry` and `locus_occupants_at_entry` are recorded empty; `locus_vacated` is false because the body is the home ship (F-14), so the due run is decided by the level alone and the breach is never voided; clause (iii) of AT-12 and the X10 clause (iv) both require a non-home body, so neither applies, and the breach ends by (i) `BREACH_MAX_TICKS` or (ii) `CLEAR_HOLD_TICKS` of quiet after `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` (the level is global, so the crew ends it by going quiet wherever they are). `set_beacon(on)` is submitted at T+1 as for any breach and sweeps on the empty home ship; STRESS lands on the home body in bursts; `TellAudit` records each deposit as an ordinary harm event inside a told breach's locus body, and the contract holds (F-23): the tell was perceivable to every living player through the non-positional bed, and Audio's alarm pulse is heard wherever they are (AA-20), so only the beacon goes unseen. A player who comes home mid-breach is not in `locus_occupants_at_entry`, changes no end clause and meets the breach (and Horror's REACH if they linger, HE-F16). **The player-facing price, stated honestly:** the noise made at home, or the lights left on at home (Moment C), comes due on the home ship whether or not anyone is there; it is the consequence of the OQ-AT-12 ruling and it was told. Web Map's request to hold the transfer until a seat is aboard (ASK-A4) is declined (AT-22). Criterion: AC-AT-46 clause 4.
- **E-24. If a tell is the shortest possible** (`attention_arm_tell` with 600): the beats follow F-12 (at least 4 in the window on any level trajectory, 5 when the level stays at or above the hold line) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, the tier follows F-13, and the BREACH-or-abort decision still occurs at the due run. R10 (b) binds at this length, and at ring 3 with the placeholder dark load it holds with 24 ticks of slack, 6 with the two wake glows (F-15).
- **E-25. If the player enters TELL at the highest possible level and does everything right** (stops, sets any cargo down, kills the lamp, opens the breaker): the abort completes inside the shortest tell in play (F-15b slack 144 to 174 ticks, 126 at ring 3 with the two wake glows) but only if the reaction allowance holds; a player who reacts after `REACT_ALLOW_TICKS` forfeits the abort on the shortest draws. That is the stated price of attention, not a defect (AT-10).
- **E-26. If the player stops everything at `theta` and later tries to resume the interrupted cut**: the abort completes 594 ticks after the stop on a clean ship (F-15b), 6 ticks inside `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`, a window no player can use, and 606 ticks with the two shipped wake glows, past it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: the partial is **always** forfeited and the cut restarts from zero *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Restraint costing the partial is a legitimate price (AT stance on Moment A).

#### Breach, locus, STRESS and the beacon

- **E-27. If the locus section is cut away or goes dormant during TELL or BREACH** *(provisional — Assembly; Web Map is designed)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (a departure that empties a non-home locus is resolved by the void of AT-22 first and never reaches this fallback: during a TELL the fallback is suppressed from the first run at which `locus_vacated` holds, so the dormancy of a departed derelict at run N does not re-point the locus before the due run N+k): the locus falls back to the home-ship's lowest `section_id`, `locus_body` is recomputed, the tell is not restarted and `tell_remaining` is unchanged; TellAudit records `LOCUS_FALLBACK(epoch, old_body, new_body, tick)` and the contract evaluates later deposits against the new body (F-23, AT-15); STRESS already armed on a cut-away section lapses on its own hold. **A cut moves the locus and never launders Attention** (Modular Ship Assembly E-26, T15 A1): the level `L`, `phase`, the timers and `tell_epoch` are unchanged by the cut, and only the cut-away section's ledger entry is dropped. In BREACH, a dormancy caused by a pod departure that empties a non-home locus is resolved by the AT-22 departure test first (it ends the breach, WM:87), so the fallback applies only when the breach is not ended that run.
- **E-28. If two sections tie in `ledger_mAU` at TELL entry**: the lowest `section_id` is the locus (a deterministic tie-break, F-6).
- **E-29. If a STRESS deposit is dropped because `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` is saturated in the run** (Horror deposits by path (a), Class B `deposit_trace` from the `horror.step` hook, so Attention claims no first place, C22, Horror HE-28): Attention cannot observe the drop (the mutator is Class B and returns no rejection), so the section is unstressed until the next burst run, one burst period (`STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS` × 6 = 1,800 ticks) later at most. Missing STRESS is never an unannounced harm (STRESS only amplifies failures that were already ticking), so the tell-window contract is not violated; the guarantee against the drop is Horror's must-repeat budget of at most `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP − STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` per run.
- **E-30. If `set_beacon` is rejected** (`NOT_LIVE` because the home-ship's power is dead, `NO_SUCH_EMITTER`, `NOT_BEACON`) or expires at `max_age_ticks`: the machine proceeds (the bed, STRESS and the failing hardware carry the breach), the failure is logged once, and the command is retried once per run while `beacon_retry_left > 0` (`BEACON_RETRY_RUNS` placeholder 20). A dead ship's beacon is dark (EM:200); this is intended.
- **E-31. If the beacon's own draw reaches the power channel in BREACH or SUBSIDING** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: it cannot. `bcn_main` is a LAMP node, so its 20 W and its switch-on step sit inside `lamps_lit_w` and `nl_w` excludes them (F-4, LS-19); the alarm cannot hold `L` above `theta_HOLD` and delay the exit it is tested against. If a future Graph revision stopped counting `bcn_main` as a lamp, the 20 W would add 12 mAU per run steady plus 720 mAU per run for five runs (`(6000 × 20 × 6) div 1000`): the lint `BEACON_NOT_LAMP` (AC-AT-63) reports it, and it is never a silent behaviour change.
- **E-32. If the beacon is switched on at BREACH entry and the listener or the nearest player is on a derelict**: no visible beacon reaches them (one beacon, on the home-ship, DA-24); the held rim, the failing hardware and Audio's alarm pulse on its fallback clock carry the breach (the tell bed ended at BREACH entry, Audio AA-20), and under reduced flashing the beacon is a static mark (AT-18, AT-24).
- **E-33. If a breach's locus body is a non-home body with no living player at BREACH entry** (a lit derelict kept loaded by a berth after the crew left it; X10 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*): BREACH ends at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS`; STRESS lapses on the Graph's hold; the beacon goes off at SUBSIDING entry. The clause is evaluated at entry only, so a player who arrives later is not retroactively rescued and, not being in `locus_occupants_at_entry`, does not trigger clause (iii) by leaving; clause (iii) is for an occupant recorded at entry.
- **E-34. If STRESS outlasts SUBSIDING** (tail of 606 to 2,400 ticks, F-18): no new Attention exogenous call occurs in CALM (the contract predicate is satisfied by the existing arming), and the ship keeps failing faster for the tail. This is the intended "spreading wrongness, then silence" (LSG:986); no Attention surface shows the tail and `phase == CALM` is never an all-clear (Audio must repeat).
- **E-35. If a breach lands on a clean ship** (no endogenous fault in the locus body): STRESS multiplies nothing and the breach is nearly a non-event. This cannot be the normal MVP case: every possible locus body on MVP derelicts A and B is authored with at least one active overload or fatigue fault (AT-11, the gate's content requirement; AC-AT-117), and Horror's single MVP behaviour wired to BREACH is a blocking dependency. A body the player has repaired clean is their earned result, and `BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT` is not raised to hide it.
- **E-36. If the cryo bay (the EMERGENCY fixture `fx_cryo_lamp`) holds the locus or the player is in it during a tell**: the lamp counts as light in F-3, never gutters and never carries a tier (LO-20, DA-23); filaments and the audio bed carry the tell, and no outcome changes.
- **E-37. If the locus body has more sections than one burst can cover** (`S > STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS × STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`): the second lint of F-18 fails at authoring; at run time the cursor wraps and the remaining sections wait for the next burst. The MVP's largest body (14) is covered by 24.

#### Degenerate strategies

- **E-38. If the player stands still in the dark indefinitely, holding no cargo** (CR-DOM; with cargo see E-66) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: the level settles at `x_dark` (F-16), no tell can start, and nothing progresses, because every repair, cut and weld verb is lit-only (IA-6), the loops run on the survival load that is part of `x_dark`, and the breaker's inrush is the price of ever lighting up again. The web is not toothless: the cost of restraint is time, air and heat, not an Attention penalty. A playtest tripwire (abort share above 85 percent among experienced players, AC-GD-16) is the signal that the freeze has become a ritual.
- **E-39. If a player baits tells and aborts them for free** (tell farming): an abort leaves `L` unchanged (`ABORT_RELIEF_PM` 1000), so the next crossing costs the same effort as the first, and `TELL_REARM_TICKS` forbids re-entry for 600 ticks. The knob `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` below 1000 exists only as a lever if playtests show chained-abort fatigue.
- **E-40. If a player throws a glowstick into an empty section to steer the locus** (CR-LURE) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* the stick's light enters that section's ledger like any light (F-2, F-6): 96 mAU per tick, a steady ledger of 172,800 mAU (691,200 at the stack cap of four), against steady ledgers of 2,318,400 for a player walking with the beam, 518,400 for one standing still with the beam and about 1,379,000 for the lit idle home ship. **Noise dominates the ledger, and a lit stick left alone wins the locus only against sections quieter still** (a full stack out-weighs a player standing still with only the beam, and only while the home ship is dark). A stick that lands in another section of the same body changes nothing, because STRESS covers the whole locus body (AT-14); it can move the breach only by landing in a different loaded body. The landing push (180,000 mAU, a one-shot `LOUD`, IA-33) is attributed to the section where it lands and decays out of that ledger with `tau`; the global level rose by the same push. A playtest watch item together with CR-DOM; `LOCUS_OCC_PREF_PM` is the lever.
- **E-41. If a player splits a long job across pauses to launder noise**: IR-3 keeps `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` at most twice `TAU_ATT_TICKS`, so a job split across the TTL retains about 72 percent of its first block; there is no pause length that resets the level to `B` except the decay itself.
- **E-42. If a player skims the threshold** (repeated entry and abort at the edge): the 600-tick rearm and the unchanged level make each cycle cost a full decay and a full re-approach; onset cues cannot chatter (AUD X10 adopted).
- **E-43. If a player runs continuously with the beam on**: the level crosses `theta` from `B` in 2,412 ticks (40 s) in the integer model and the tell begins; this is the intended punishment for refusing the one noise readout the game gives (the footsteps).
- **E-44. If an Assembly weld completes in a derelict welded to the home ship**: **zero** `LOUD` is pushed at weld completion (IA-33 declares `LOUD` one-shots at cut completion, seam-cut DONE, vent apply and throw landing only; a weld completion is none of these; the weld's cost to Attention is its accrued `TOOL` ticks), Assembly pushes nothing at MVP, and the locus body now spans both, so the next breach shares STRESS across the welded body. A seam-cut reaching DONE pushes exactly one `LOUD`, from Interaction (Modular Ship Assembly T14 C5).
- **E-65. If a player throttles** (alternates short loud bursts with long stillness so the level hovers under `theta` and no tell ever starts) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: no tell, no breach and no Horror content ever occurs, `breaches per session` falls toward 0 (below the registered 3 to 8) and the build is toothless in the way a hidden-timer design is not. E-38 covers the pure freeze, whose cost is air, heat and time; throttling is cheaper because it keeps working. **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions), OQ-AT-27: lever (a), the calibration route**, delivered by the carry-burden term of F-26 (the weights and `ATT_THRESHOLD` are retained; an ordinary haul now reaches `theta`), so a hauling sortie cannot be throttled under the line; throttle play on work that carries nothing stays a playtest watch item behind the AC-AT-80 gate. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **A second route to near-zero breaches, from the rulings themselves:** the departure void (OQ-AT-12) plus the carry burden (X3) make walking to the pod the near-dominant answer to any derelict tell when the pod is within about 37 tiles unladen (about 19 tiles carrying; 900 ticks at 2 and 1 px per tick) (a hop voids the breach and keeps the cut and the haul; an abort forfeits the partial), and a void leaves the level unrelieved but the level decays through the pod, because the burden stops at the pod door (AT-3): on the reference carry it arrives home at 4,511,793 mAU lit (3,734,036 with no pod intake), below `theta_HOLD` 4,800,000, so no home tell opens (Boarding EC-BS-63, AC-BS-123 (e)); a hop made earlier in a tell, at a higher level, can be followed by a home-ship tell once the rearm ends, and AC-AT-134 measures how often (Moment C, E-73) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. Restraint aborts and breaches then both trend toward zero, which threatens Pillar 3 and the AC-AT-80 band. It is a consequence of two user rulings, not a reason to reopen either; it is not retuned silently: AC-AT-134 measures it and OQ-AT-36 is decided (leave it, measured; R21) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*. The levers considered, none of which may become a hidden timer: (a) calibrate the weights and `ATT_THRESHOLD` so an ordinary sortie reaches `theta` (the AC-AT-57 harness picks), (b) the Vertical Slice per-ring gain on light and power (AT-6), (c) a Graph-side cost that grows with time spent working (air, heat), which is visible and diegetic. Detection: the AC-AT-80 breaches-per-session gate.

#### Carry, freeze and the user rulings of 2026-10-27 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

- **E-66. If the player holds cargo and stands still, in the light or in the dark** *(user ruling X3)*: the Controller keeps pushing `BURDEN` every tick while the cargo is salvage (F-26), and the decay is suspended while it is held (F-5), so standing still is not a way to wait the web out *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*. Lit, with the beam on and the home ship lit, the level crosses `theta` in 360 ticks after the cut and 540 ticks from the lit-idle level; fully dark (beam off, home dark, start at the 829 AU dark floor) it crosses in 714 ticks. The escapes are the ones the rules name: set the cargo down (`drop` is free and exempt from the lit rule; the item stays where it fell and re-taking it needs light, IA-28; re-taking the same item, or carrying it back aboard a non-home body, before the rearm ends cancels the rearm, AT-10 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*) or ride the tell out. Cargo carried at home, or the player's own kit, is not salvage: it pushes no burden and suspends nothing. The freeze after the cut is priced, not free (OQ-BS-27). E-38's pure freeze (no cargo) is unchanged.
- **E-67. If the level dips under `theta_HOLD` and climbs back before the abort** *(user ruling OQ-AT-25)*: `below_hold_runs` resets (E-17), `esc_ticks` stays where the dip left it and resumes from there, `tier` and the beat interval never fall, and beats keep publishing in real time at the frozen interval; one dip neither aborts the tell nor makes it worse.
- **E-68. If a restore changes `restore_epoch`** *(user ruling D16)*: a tell already drawn in the snapshot keeps its `tell_len` and replays its beats; the next `draw_epoch(attention.tell, rng_counter)` through the `RngFacet` keys the stream with the new epoch (the facet's `counter_eff`, never computed by Attention) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0005 sync)*, so it differs from the one the earlier run met; `rng_counter` itself is restored exactly. Every death-stub restore advances the epoch, and so does a Continue (in-process or fresh-process) of any generation other than the newest Save & Quit generation, so a second death restore of the same autosave and a Continue of an autosave after a fresh process start both take a new epoch; Save/Load makes the advance durable in the `slot_epoch_a/b.bin` pair *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0006 sync)* before `request_restore`. A Continue of that newest `QUIT` generation carries the epoch and replays the same draws (R1 as amended by R13/R14) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R1)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. A restore with the same epoch value pinned (the test harness) is hash-identical to uninterrupted play (AC-AT-22).
- **E-69. If the last living seat dies in the pod** *(user ruling D60)*: no section contains a living player, `B` holds its last value (E-63) and the death handling restores an autosave (E-22); a seat that dies in the pod while another lives at home stops counting for `B` and the pod hold, and the departure clauses (AT-12 iii, AT-22) already held because its section had left `locus_body` (its `occ_gone` was set while it was `IN_POD` and is kept after death, F-14). A recorded seat that dies **inside** the locus body is the opposite case: it keeps counting as present, so a death never voids a tell or ends a breach early *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **E-70. If the pod is berthed in the locus body, or in a derelict where nothing else is lit** *(user ruling X2)*: the DOCK_STATUS glow is lit, counts as light (F-3) and as unswitchable (F-16), and never carries a strand (AT-20); a site lit only by it is not eligible, so a deaf player in that derelict has no visual channel and the opt-in haptic pulse is theirs (AT-24).
- **E-71. If a berth completes in the run that crosses `theta`, or while a tell runs** *(default applied, D59)*: the one `LOUD` push (180,000 mAU, 3 percent of `theta`) is part of that run's `I_run` like any other (E-12); it cannot open a tell from the lit-idle level on its own, and it is attributed to the home-ship console section, so it adds to that section's ledger and not to the derelict's.
- **E-72. If the haptic pulse is on and the game restores, pauses or hitches** *(user ruling OQ-AT-1)*: the pulse fires on the `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` change only, so a load fires none (transitions only, AT-27), a pause fires none and a hitch fires at most one pulse for the current pair (the dedupe of AC-AT-115).

#### Save, restore and persistence

- **E-45. If a snapshot is restored mid-TELL**: `rng_counter`, `tell_len`, `breach_due_T`, `tell_beat`, `esc_ticks`, `beat_next_off` and `below_hold_runs` restore, no length is redrawn, no onset cue replays (cues fire on transitions only), and the beat sequence is hash-identical to uninterrupted play (G4; AT-1, AT-27).
- **E-46. If a snapshot is restored mid-BREACH**: `stress_next_burst_run` and `stress_pending_cursor` restore, bursts resume on schedule, and no deposit gap exceeds the coverage margin; the beacon is reconciled by the first run (E-48).
- **E-47. If a snapshot lands on the `breach_due_T` run**: both the pre-run state (phase TELL) and the post-run state (phase BREACH) restore to an identical continuation, because the transition is inside one run and `phase` changes nowhere else.
- **E-48. If `beacon_commanded` disagrees with the emitter's switch state after a restore** (a SYSTEM command pending at snapshot time is dropped on reload, KRN:593, :609): `post_restore` changes nothing and submits nothing; the first `attention.step` run after the restore compares the two and submits `set_beacon` only on a mismatch, with the usual `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS` retry. If they already agree, nothing is submitted (idempotent).
- **E-49. If a save made in CALM with `tell_outcome = ABORTED`, or in BREACH, is loaded**: no onset, no breach-enter and no release cue plays (transition-only); the view carries the persisted `tell_outcome` and `tell_epoch`.
- **E-50. If a loaded block holds an out-of-range value, an incoherent phase or an unknown section id**: restore reports a hard failure through `ctx.fault(ATTENTION_RESTORE_RANGE)`, `_PHASE_INCOHERENT` or `_SECTION_UNKNOWN` (the kernel fires `load_failed`, which Save/Load maps to the `RESTORE_FAILED` class) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0006 sync)*; nothing is clamped and the load is refused (SL-15).
- **E-51. If an autosave is due during a TELL**: Save/Load's gate reads `tell_active`, `tell_remaining` and `breach_phase`; because `breach_phase` reports TELL as CALM, FAIR mode stays live (C5). A state saved mid-TELL restores the full tell (`tell_len ≥ TELL_MIN_TICKS`), so the player is never saved into a shorter warning than the contract allows. The gate formula itself is Save/Load's.
- **E-52. If the game is paused or the window loses focus**: sim `T` stops and Attention runs on `T` only; no wall time is read, so no catch-up burst of beats or decay occurs on resume (AT-28). The kernel never fast-forwards (`LAG_FORGIVE_TICKS` = 0, ratified 2026-10-27 with the kernel batch, X13), so a hitch replays no missed sim ticks, and the per-catch-up-frame merge of PRESENTATION and DIAGNOSTIC notifications leaves one beat notification per poll, which is the dedupe AC-AT-115 already tests *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.

#### Hallucination interplay (the tell is a truth channel)

- **E-53. If a phantom entity lunges at a low-sanity player** (class a) with no beat, no resolving filament and no beacon before it: TellAudit records nothing (there is no web-originated event), Attention does nothing, and the player has a cheap first check that costs attention (they must have been listening to the bed): "the web did not look up, so this is not the web" (AT-17). The boundary is honest: it rules out web-originated harm, not an entity's presence and not the crew; a real entity may stand in CALM (Horror's non-lethal presence outside BREACH is required), so the check never licenses ignoring what is seen.
- **E-54. If a false system state shows a hull failing** (class b) while `phase` is CALM: no STRESS exists, the real gauge is unaffected, and `attention.view` still reports CALM; Attention reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` value, so the lie cannot reach the machine (CR-12).
- **E-55. If the Perception Filter or Sanity tries to remove, add, delay or alter a tell cue** (a filament, a bed beat): it is a lint failure (the Filter's write set must not contain an Attention cue; its anchor list includes the tell cues). The MVP has no phantom tell and no false calm (AT-17).
- **E-56. If a crewmate appears as a horror and lethal friendly harm follows** (class c): friendly harm is Sanity's gate, not Attention's; Attention neither gates nor enables it. A crewmate's tool harm is player-originated and needs no tell; only a horror's lethal act on a crew body is audited by `tell_window_contract`, provisionally (R15).
- **E-57. If a phantom emitter, a hallucinated beam or phantom loot exists in the player's view**: the emitter list holds real records only (EM-3 gives a record no phantom field), so phantom light adds nothing; taking phantom loot picks nothing up and pushes no noise. Real carry noise comes only from a real action.

#### Co-op hooks (Vertical Slice; one machine at MVP)

- **E-58. If two players stand in different rings** (VS-B): `B` uses the maximum ring over living players (F-8); each hears the non-positional bed; `tell_beat` is shared (`SHARED_SUMMARY`); the single level and the `source` tag keep per-player attribution a data change.
- **E-59. If a downed crewmate's beam or a corpse could be a lantern**: noted only (EM:1121); it counts as an emitter if the Emitter State lists it, with no Attention rule of its own.
- **E-60. If the locus body holds only some of the players**: STRESS and `exogenous_permitted` apply to the locus body alone; the other players still hear the bed (perceivable relative to every living player, AT-15) and the home-ship beacon, and are safe from the breach's STRESS unless their body is welded to the locus body.

#### Absent or provisional neighbours

- **E-61. If Web Map's ring is absent**: `ring_of` (registry, Web Map & Transit) resolves an absent or sub-1 ring to ring 1 with one log line per process (AT-6).
- **E-62. If Ship Body's `actor_section` is unavailable for a seat** *(Body is Designed, 2026-10-27)*: that player's noise enters `I_noise` and the ledger drop of E-3 applies; the foot-origin lookup never blocks the machine. For the departure tests (the void, AT-22; clause (iii), AT-12) a recorded seat whose lookup is unavailable keeps its last `occ_gone` status, present if it never left, and one diagnostic is logged per epoch, so an unavailable lookup can never void a tell or end a breach by itself (F-14) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*.
- **E-63. If the Controller's `loco_state` is DEAD for every seat**: no section "contains a living player", so `B` holds its last value; the death handling then restores an autosave (E-22). Attention never invents a ring.
- **E-64. If the crew is aboard a derelict while the home ship stays lit** (Boarding & Salvage OQ-BS-15, AC-BS-80): the home ship's light and power terms **keep feeding the one world level** (AT-2; R2). A HOME-kind section is never dormant (Web Map & Transit), so its emitters and `power_use` are read every run like any loaded section's ("off-screen and unoccupied emitters count", EM:247); the lit-home term (766 mAU per tick at the F-25 placeholders) is part of the level the crew carries on the derelict. Switching home lamps off before a sortie is the player's lever. The locus can be a home section if its ledger leads (AT-14, Moment C).

**Index corrections routed (this GDD never edits the index):** Attention ← Interaction (soft; Interaction is a push source); Attention ← Controller (soft; already noted in the index's design note); Attention ← Ship Body / Layout (soft); Attention ← Web Map & Transit (soft); Attention → Suit downgraded to soft, none at MVP; Lighting ← Attention reworded from "never reads" to "reads the published tier and timing only" (C12); the rationale bullet's "pushed in by Graph, Suit, … Assembly" (C6) and "flicker" (C10). The Dependency Map's "[bottleneck: 5 dependents]" is Horror Entities, Sanity, Adaptive Audio, Depth Rings and the Knowledge Codex; Lighting and Save/Load read Attention but are listed as dependencies elsewhere.

**Circularity check.** Attention reads the Graph's power phase (ordered `after: graph.propagate.power`) and calls its Class B mutator; the Graph reads nothing from Attention (LSG:281). Attention reads the Emitter State's list (ordered `after: emitter.place`) and submits a SYSTEM command at T+1; the Emitter State's only read of Attention is none. Lighting reads Attention's published tier and Attention reads nothing Lighting produces (LO:948), which resolves the index's soft cycle one-way. Horror's hook is `after` Attention and Attention reads nothing from Horror (Horror pushes no noise, HE-36; had it any, Class B calls inside a `WriteScope` would be inputs, not reads). Sanity reads Attention and Attention reads nothing from Sanity. The Controller pushes noise and Attention reads only `loco_state`; nothing in the Controller reads Attention. No cycle exists in the hook DAG: `suit.vitals → controller.step → body.integrate → emitter.place → attention.step`, and `graph.propagate.power → attention.step`, with Horror, Audio's polling subscriber and the Presenter downstream.

**Engine reference gaps (Godot 4.7.2, pinned; probed 2026-10-25).** `docs/engine-reference/godot/` holds `VERSION.md`, `breaking-changes.md`, `current-best-practices.md`, `deprecated-apis.md` and `modules/` (animation, audio, input, navigation, networking, physics, rendering, ui). **There is no `scripting.md` or `core.md`:** GDScript 64-bit integer arithmetic, `RefCounted` block performance, `Dictionary` iteration order and signal semantics are NOT SOURCEABLE from the reference (integer division is not on this list: it is probed on 4.7.2 and closed by ADR-0005 s1.4, which truncates toward zero and gives floor via `IntMath.floor_div` / `posmod_i`) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0006 sync)*, so every cost number in this GDD is a model parameter for a spike and every determinism claim that rests on iteration order says "written, not assumed" (section-keyed arrays stored sorted by `section_id`). The domain is a pure-integer headless SIM block, so the reference is silent on the system; `audio.md` (tell sounds) and `rendering.md` (filament drawing) were consulted. Sourced 4.7 hazards that bite: a typed-return override of `read_view()` needs an explicit `return` on every path (HIGH); a packed-array element write no longer calls the property setter, so dirty keys are tracked explicitly in the mutators (HIGH); `CanvasItem` line drawing has no antialiasing feather, so 1-art-px filaments must be verified at every zoom rung (HIGH); `AudioStreamPlayer2D.area_mask` now defaults to 0, so any area-based bus override silently does nothing until set (MEDIUM, installed editor is 4.7.2 so the risk is live); `duplicate()` is not deep (MEDIUM); the renderer default and glow ordering affect any glow-dependent iridescence (MEDIUM, the lighting spike decides). No ADR exists yet (`docs/architecture/` holds only `tr-registry.yaml`); the ADR candidates raised here are: a pure integer hash shared with Lighting for presentation randomness (closed: `Ihash.h32`, ADR-0005 section 2.6) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0005 sync)*; presentation-subscriber polling by counter comparison; the block base class and `read_view()` return discipline under 4.7; filament rendering at 1 art px; audio voice reservation and the SubViewport listener policy (Audio's).

## Tuning Knobs

*Every number in this section is a `[PLACEHOLDER]` unless a row says "derived", "fixed" or "cited". Data lives in `assets/data/attention/{intake, integrator, weights, baseline, tell, breach, presentation}.json` (the audio-side knobs live in Adaptive Audio's `assets/data/audio/tell_audio.json` and are pointers here). **★ marks a registry candidate**: a cross-system fact another GDD reads, cites or must keep consistent (the registry obligations are listed in Cross-References). "Safe range" is the range the lints of Formulas allow at the placeholder rows, and it is guidance: only a schema bound (`CONTENT_FIELD_RANGE`) or a named invariant (`CONTENT_INVARIANT`) refuses a load, so an edge that no lint binds is advisory only (ADR-0007 s2.5; AC-AT-106 classes (a) and (b)) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix)*; where a lint, not taste, sets the edge (that edge refuses boot with `CONTENT_INVARIANT`), the edge is computed with the integer model of F-15 at the **ring-3 test row** (the tightest row: `B` 550 AU with the two wake glows, `x_dark` 1,209 AU, 98 runs against a limit of 99). Each row names its owner in parentheses: SD systems-designer, GD game-designer, AD audio-director, TD technical-director, ART art-director.*

#### Intake and the unit table

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `U_TOOL` ★ (SD; `intake`) | 4 | AU per accrued tool tick | 2 to 6 (IR-1) | tool work is as loud as running ×1.5; every cut is a tell | tool verbs feel free; no cost to working | IR-1, IR-2, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`; the 180-tick patch = 720 AU |
| `U_LOUD` ★ (SD; `intake`) | 180 (= 30 × 6) | AU per one-shot | `30 × U_TOOL` to `ATT_THRESHOLD` ÷ 10 (600 at theta 6000, 720 at 7200), so the **GD alternative 600** (CQ-6) is inside the range at both thresholds (it sat outside the earlier fixed 400) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | one bang is a threshold event, nothing else matters | a bang is 3.0 percent of theta (180 of 6,000) and inert | IR-2, the chain-crossing trace (F-25), `ATT_THRESHOLD` |

**Safe ranges by row** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (the single-knob edges of the integer model, two wake glows; each knob varied alone, the others at their defaults; the ring-3 column is the one the tables above quote):

| Knob (default) | MVP ring 1, switches guarantee (F-15, home dark) | MVP ring 1, anywhere guarantee (F-15b, home lit, no transient) | Ring-3 test row (switches) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `TAU_ATT_TICKS` (1800) | up to 1,910 | up to 1,848 | up to 1,816 |
| `HOLD_PM` (800) | from 787 | from 793 | from 798 |
| `L_CEIL_PM` (1020) | up to 1,039 | up to 1,030 | up to 1,022 |
| `ATT_MARGIN` (50 AU) | up to 132 | up to 93 | up to 62 |
| `W_P` (100) | up to 209 | up to 139 | up to 116 |
| `ATT_THRESHOLD` (6000 AU), lower edge | from 4,767 | from 5,618 | from 5,816 |

The lower and upper edges that no lint and no schema bound binds (for example `L_CEIL_PM` below 1,005) keep the ranges printed in the knob rows as guidance only. `W_P` above 300 is not one of them: 300 is the schema bound of the `weights` field, so 301 refuses with `CONTENT_FIELD_RANGE` *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix, round 3)*. The `autonomous_transient` row has no single-knob edge at the placeholders; it needs the OQ-AT-26 retune.

*Interaction notes.* (1) The ring-3 test row is deliberately near the cliff of F-15: tau 1,817, `HOLD_PM` 797, `L_CEIL_PM` 1,023, `ATT_MARGIN` 63, `W_P` 117 and a ring-3 baseline of 595 AU each fail it singly (with the two wake glows), so several of these cannot move together; the lint, not this table, is the authority. (2) `ATT_THRESHOLD` and `U_LOUD` trade off: at 6000 and 180 the AC-AT-57f chain trace crosses in about 50 seconds from the home-idle level (about 60 seconds from `B`; integer F-5 run, 3,000 ticks); the game-designer's 7200 and 600 cross at the same fifth job end on that fixture (about 50 seconds) with a larger span; the harness fixes the pair. (3) The occupancy lever order if playtests show a siege: `REFRACT_TICKS`, then `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, never `BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT`. (4) The candidates with a ★ are registered in Cross-References; none of the Graph's, the Controller's or the Emitter's constants is.

## Visual/Audio Requirements

*Lane.* Attention defines **no sound and no sprite**. It publishes facts (AT-16) and owns one presenter (AT-20); the pixels of the beacon belong to Emitter State and the Derelict Authoring kit, the lamp gutter to 2D Lighting, the failure trails to Failure & Trail, and every sound, mix and cue name to Adaptive Audio & Tells (Designed, `adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`: every audio line below is a **requirement on that GDD**, which repeats it in its own AA rules). This section is the art-director's and audio-director's proposals folded into one set. **Requirement ids.** `AV-n` (visual) and `AUD-n` (audio) are *requirements under AT-9, AT-20, AT-24 and AT-26*, not Core Rules; they keep the specialists' ids so a citation into their spec files stays legible. Every presenter number below is an **art value `[PLACEHOLDER]`**, lives in `assets/data/attention/presentation.json`, is not a simulation constant, is not a registry entry and never feeds `state_hash` (changing one never invalidates a save). Art-bible citations are by section id (`ART 2.7`) with the lines used elsewhere in this GDD (`ART:741`, `:954`).

*Orchestrator and gate rulings in this section.* (1) *(gate ruling, CD-GDD-ALIGN, supersedes the earlier orchestrator ruling "inside a lit cone": strands resolve at the edge of the lit region of a player-controllable real emitter, kinds `BEAM`, `FIXTURE`, `NODE_LANTERN` and `DEPLOYABLE`; `BEACON`, the cryo EMERGENCY lamp `fx_cryo_lamp`, the DOCK_STATUS pod glow (X2) and `FAULT_GLOW` are excluded; strands are never drawn in unlit space. Cone-only is a producer scope fallback only. Every "lit region" below is this reading; AT-20 holds the definition. Consequence: with the chest lamp off but fixtures lit the visual channel remains; with nothing lit there is none, which `ART:954` accepts.)* (2) *(orchestrator ruling: strand counts are the Tuning Knobs `FILAMENT_STRANDS[tier]` 2 / 4 / 7 per lit region, which sit inside the art-director's bands 1 to 2 / 3 to 5 / 6 to 9; `STRAND_VIEW_CAP` 9 is the hard ceiling.*) (3) *(orchestrator ruling: hull-stress hairlines are cut from the Tell permanently, not only from MVP, as the art-director recommends; a Vertical Slice revisit is allowed only if the hairline can be made unconfusable with both the Integrity trail and a hidden-entity footprint, ART 5.5 (v).)*

### Events: what the player sees and hears

*Reading key.* "Source" is the published fact the presenter or Audio reads (`attention.view`, AT-16). **Class T** is the truth channel (AT-17): derived from SIM fields, never from `PERCEPTION_OUT`, never added, removed, delayed or altered by the Perception Filter or Sanity. "Owner" names who authors the pixels or the sound; Attention owns only the rows marked *Presenter*. Priority: **P0** = required for the MVP contract (a tell the player could have perceived), **P1** = required for MVP feel, **P2** = polish or cuttable.

| # | Event (source) | Visual feedback | Audio feedback (Audio's design; Attention's requirement) | Owner (visual / audio) | Class | Priority |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| V1 | **CALM below the cue floor** (`level_pm` below `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM`) | Nothing Attention-specific. The ring's resident web is exactly as the ring table (ART 4.3) says | Ordinary ship ambience only; no creak keyed to the level | Environment kit, Lighting / Audio | T | P0 |
| V2 | **CALM pre-tell step 1..`CUE_STEPS`** (`level_pm` quantised, `CUE_HYST_PM` on the way down; AT-26) | A few **stubs** at web roots at the rim of the lit region: about 40 percent of a full strand, thick-rooted, **static** (no band-slide, no twitch). Step *n* raises the stub count in whole sites. A step change reveals or hides stubs in a 3-frame stepped reveal (12 fps), never a fade, never a pop on a lit pixel | Hull-settling creaks, more frequent per step (event id distinct from tell beats; excluded from `TellAudit` and the floor-window count); no pitched rising tone, no drone keyed to the level | Presenter / Audio | T | P1 |
| V3 | **TELL entry, beat 0** (`phase` CALM to TELL, `tell_beat` 0, published in the entry run; AT-9) | The **onset**: in every lit region the nearest unresolved site to the rim resolves from stub or hidden to a full strand with the **band-slide** (AV-5); existing stubs grow in the same 4 stepped frames; the dust in the beam takes its first deflection impulse. **The presenter draws nothing for an epoch before beat 0 is published** (audio-first, ART 7.6 / HR-9) | A distinct onset event, the unique beat with `tell_beat == 0` ("it noticed", ART 2.7): one unmistakable moment and a learnable signature. The non-positional structure-borne **bed** starts, audible to every living player | Presenter / Audio | T | P0 |
| V4 | **Each further beat** (`(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` increases; `tell_beat_T`) | Exactly one visual beat: in every lit region at least one strand resolves (the guaranteed resolve, AV-7) with the band-slide; sites whose hash falls between the old and the new density threshold resolve with it; one dust impulse. Rate ceiling (derived): `TICK_RATE / BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS` = 2 events per second, under the 3 Hz ceiling | One creak beat in the bed, at most one per poll; positional creak layer anchored at `locus_section_id` (Audio picks the hull anchor from Layout) | Presenter / Audio | T | P0 |

| V6 | **Lamp gutter** (`tier` at least 1, read by Lighting, LO-18 to LO-20) | On FIXTURE (never the cryo EMERGENCY lamp) and NODE_LANTERN only: brief dips of output, irregular, never a colour or temperature change, never an overshoot, never to out. Spacing and depth are Lighting's recipe; Attention supplies the tier only. An optional **third** channel (C2: the spacing can give zero dips in a floor-length tell, so the contract does not lean on it) | none | Lighting | T | P2 |

No beacon, no colour, no glyph or flourish for "success". No band-slide (a release must never read as a beat) | A release cue (the bed decays; beats stop), distinct in event id and spectral identity from the subside cue; its length equals `STRAND_RELEASE_TICKS` so ear and eye agree | Presenter / Audio | T | P0 |
| V8 | **BREACH entry** (`phase` TELL to BREACH; `breach_enter_T`; AT-11) | The **snap-taut**: every resolved strand pulls straight in `TAUT_SNAP_FRAMES` stepped frames and **holds absolutely still** (the held rim, AV-10); dust deflection ends. The beacon lens is lit one tick later (`set_beacon` at T+1). The STRESS-driven trail changes are the Graph's consequences, drawn by Failure & Trail | A breach-entry event bounded in loudness step from the last beat (must-repeat M4); the alarm cadence starts (M1) | Presenter (rim, dust); Emitter State / Lighting (beacon); Failure & Trail (trails) / Audio | T | P0 |

| V11 | **SUBSIDING to CALM** | No event. The rim has relaxed to the CALM step the level supports (V1 or V2). No end-of-breach flourish; the refractory is invisible | none | Presenter / Audio | T | P1 |

A pause stops everything (sim `T`) | The bed fades in over `RESTORE_FADE_TICKS` and resumes at the next published beat; no onset, breach-enter or release cue fires on the load; no catch-up burst | Presenter / Audio | T | P0 |
