[AT-8, AT-6, F-15b]
- **AC-AT-131 (data-lint) The DOCK_STATUS budget.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN `g_dock` at the budget `DOCK_GLOW_TERM_MAX` 24 and the shipped ring rows with the two wake glows, WHEN the F-15 lint runs, THEN the rows are 94 / 97 / 99 runs and pass, and the glow is counted in `r_dark` at every row; negative fixture: `g_dock` 30 gives 94 / 97 / 100 and fails the ring-3 test row with `ATT_DARK_FLOOR_RING3`. The glow's own value stays `[PLACEHOLDER — derive at spike]` until Emitter and Authoring publish it, and the shipped rows are evaluated at `g_dock` 0 until then. [F-16, X2]
- **AC-AT-17 (auto) The four-guard entry.** GIVEN `L' >= theta`, WHEN exactly one of {phase not CALM, `T < refract_until_T`, `T < rearm_until_T`, `L' < theta`} is true, THEN no TELL starts; WHEN none is true (including `T == refract_until_T`), THEN TELL starts. [AT-7, F-9, E-14, E-15]
- **AC-AT-85 (auto) Baseline and ring.** GIVEN seats in ring-1 and ring-2 sections, WHEN `B` is computed, THEN it is `B_table[2]`; WHEN the ring-2 seat dies or leaves, THEN `B` falls and `L` decays toward the new floor with no reset; WHEN `B` rises above `L`, THEN `L` becomes `B` in the same run, `level_pm` is 0 and no TELL starts; a ring unresolved by Web Map or the record hint falls to ring 1 and logs once per process. [AT-6, F-8, E-8, E-9, E-58, E-61]
- **AC-AT-119 (auto, Vertical Slice; deferred: blocked on Depth Rings & Scarcity) Depth is a measurable lever.** GIVEN the per-ring light and power stimulus gain data and one recorded behaviour trace (identical per-tick inputs), WHEN F-5 is integrated from each ring's baseline with the trace replayed at ring 1 and at ring 3, THEN the ring-3 level reaches `theta` in measurably fewer ticks than at ring 1, by at least `DEPTH_LEVER_MIN_PM` of the ring-1 crossing time (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 100 permille; Depth Rings & Scarcity owns and registers it), and `I_noise` is identical at both rings tick by tick (the gain is never applied to noise). If Depth Rings cannot satisfy this it surfaces a CONFLICT, it does not silently weaken the lever. [AT-6, P3, P5]
- **AC-AT-126 (data-lint) The upper bound on theta.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the integrator data and the intake data, WHEN loaded, THEN the integer F-5 run of the sustained verbs with the beam (`r_sustain` 4,288 mAU per tick) from `B` at ring 1 crosses `theta` within `CROSS_MAX_TICKS` (2,412 ticks at theta 6,000; 4,038 at 7,200 passes; 4,272 at 7,300 passes); negative fixtures: theta 7,400 (4,542) and theta 8,000 (10,872) fail with the named error `ATT_THRESHOLD_UNREACHABLE`; theta 8,100 never crosses. `U_LOUD` above `ATT_THRESHOLD ÷ 10` refuses boot. [F-5, F-22, Tuning Knobs]
- **AC-AT-86 (auto) The published level.** GIVEN `L` 3,000,000 and `B` 300,000, WHEN `level_pm` is computed, THEN it is 473; at `L` = `B` it is 0; at `L >= theta` it is 1000. [F-7, E-10]
- **AC-AT-104 (auto) Empty world.** GIVEN no loaded section, WHEN runs execute, THEN `I_light` and `I_power` are 0, `L` reaches `B` exactly and no TELL starts. [E-6]
- **AC-AT-105 (auto) Pinned Moment A trace.** GIVEN ring 1, `N_GLOW_DARK` 0 and a player who enters at `theta` and zeroes every input, WHEN runs execute, THEN `L` leaves `theta_HOLD` after 80 runs and the abort completes 594 ticks after the stop; the test asserts those numbers at the placeholder data and records them against `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` (600), so a retune that moves the 6-tick resume margin shows as a visible diff. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the **shipping fixture** (`N_GLOW_DARK` 2, `r_dark` 366), THEN `L` leaves `theta_HOLD` after 82 runs and the abort completes at 606 ticks; the pass condition on the shipping fixture is the **sign of (abort time − `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`)**: positive (the partial is forfeited) passes, zero or negative (a resume window exists) fails and goes to the creative-director, because that would change the stated price of restraint. The 594 is 480 ticks to cross `theta_HOLD` plus 114 ticks of hold (the first run under the line counts as run 1, so the hold adds 19 runs); an abort therefore always forfeits the partial, the deliberate price of restraint (the CR-TIMELINE stance, F-15b) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [F-15b, E-26]
- **AC-AT-106 (data-lint) Knob ranges and cliffs** *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix: split into two classes by what binds the edge)*. Published safe ranges are guidance (ADR-0007 s2.5); only a schema bound (`CONTENT_FIELD_RANGE`) or a named invariant (`CONTENT_INVARIANT`) refuses a load, and never a clamp. GIVEN each knob in Tuning Knobs, WHEN set one step outside its published safe range, THEN the class of the edge decides the outcome. **(a) Invariant-bound edges** (the edge is set by a named lint): one step outside refuses boot with `CONTENT_INVARIANT`, and the value one step inside passes. This class is: the six cliff values (`TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1817, `HOLD_PM` 797, `L_CEIL_PM` 1023, `ATT_MARGIN` 63, `W_P` 117, a ring-3 baseline of 595 AU), each applied alone at the ring-3 test row with the two wake glows, which fails the F-15 lint; the same for the ring-1 columns of the Safe-ranges-by-row table (switches: `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1,911, `HOLD_PM` 786, `L_CEIL_PM` 1,040, `ATT_MARGIN` 133, `W_P` 210; anywhere: 1,849, 792, 1,031, 94, 140) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the `ATT_THRESHOLD` lower edge (the ring-1 and ring-3 columns of that table) and upper edge (the `reckless_crossing` lint, F-5); `TELL_DRAW_LO` below the AC-AT-64 abortability edge (774 at ring 3 with the two wake glows, 756 clean; the knob row's printed 780 is guidance, so 779 is advisory-only while 773 refuses; negative fixture 750); `B_table` and `DOCK_GLOW_TERM_MAX` at their F-15 / F-16 edges; `W_L[BEACON]` other than 0; the AC-AT-52 knobs; and the intake relations IR-1 to IR-3 (AC-AT-03 to AC-AT-05), whose edges refuse boot with `CONTENT_INVARIANT`: `U_TOOL` 1 and 7 refuse while 2 and 6 pass (IR-1, negative fixture `U_TOOL` 7), `U_LOUD` below `30 × U_TOOL` refuses (IR-2) and `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` above `2 × TAU_ATT_TICKS` refuses (IR-3) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix, round 3: `U_TOOL` and the IR-2/IR-3 edges moved here from class (b))*. A schema-bound edge also refuses and is not a class (b) edge: `W_P` 301 refuses with `CONTENT_FIELD_RANGE` (the hard range 0 to 300 of the `weights` field), and `W_P` 300 passes the schema (it still fails F-15 at the ring-3 test row from 117). **(b) All other edges** (the guidance-only rows that no schema bound and no named lint binds, for example `TAU_ATT_TICKS` below 1200 (IR-3 does not bind until 300), `HOLD_PM` above 900, `L_CEIL_PM` below 1005 and `REACT_ALLOW_TICKS` below 120): one step outside gives the ADVISORY CI diff only and boot does not refuse, unless a schema bound (`CONTENT_FIELD_RANGE`) or an invariant (a class-(a) lint firing at that value) applies. A test must not assert refusal for a class-(b) edge. (The Tuning Knobs row is the authority for the printed range: `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1200 to 1816 at the ring-3 test row with the two wake glows, where 1200 is guidance and 1817 is the F-15 invariant; the `TAU_ATT_TICKS` figure 300, where IR-3 binds, is the formula's arithmetic domain, not the knob range. A production MVP load with ring 1 only refuses at the ring-1 columns, not the ring-3 values, because rings 2 and 3 are test rows.) [Tuning Knobs, F-15, F-16]

### D. The tell

- **AC-AT-18 (auto) A restore does not change the length.** GIVEN a snapshot taken mid-TELL, WHEN restored and continued, THEN `tell_len`, `breach_due_T`, `rng_counter`, `tell_beat`, `esc_ticks` and `beat_next_off` equal those of uninterrupted play. [AT-8, E-45]
- **AC-AT-19 (auto) The arming seam.** GIVEN `attention_arm_tell`, WHEN called in CALM with `tell_len` 1800, THEN TELL enters at the next run with beat 0 published in that run and `rng_counter` unchanged; with 100 it is clamped to 600; outside CALM it is rejected `NOT_CALM`; a PLAYER-origin submission is rejected with the kernel's origin reason. [AT-21, F-10, E-19]
- **AC-AT-99 (auto + review) The arming seam ignores the timers.** GIVEN CALM with `rearm_until_T` and `refract_until_T` in the future, WHEN `attention_arm_tell {900}` is submitted by SYSTEM, THEN TELL enters at the next run (the CALM requirement still binds); review: the command is listed in the control manifest as non-gameplay. [AT-21, F-9, E-19]
- **AC-AT-20 (auto) The two-channel floor, headless half.** GIVEN a jitter provider injected into the schedule (dependency injection per the coding standard; production uses the integer hash) forced to all +`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS`, all -`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS` and all 0, plus the real hash over `tell_epoch` 1 to 10,000, for every `tell_len` in {600, 606, ..., 1800} and a run-phase offset of 0 to 5 ticks, WHEN the schedule runs un-aborted with the level pinned at or above `theta_HOLD` (the frozen-clock trajectories are AC-AT-128), THEN at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` beats are published in `[tell_start_T, tell_start_T + TELL_MIN_TICKS)`. The recorded minimum (5 at the placeholders: offsets 0, 158, 307, 448, 580) is informational: a retune that lowers it to 4 still passes and one that lowers it to 3 fails. The test needs the injection seam (flagged to the lead-programmer). [AT-9, F-12]
- **AC-AT-21 (auto) Beat spacing.** GIVEN any epoch, WHEN beats publish, THEN nominal offsets are non-decreasing by at least `BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS`; with every jitter forced to -`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS` the smallest gap is `max(BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS, BEAT_END_TICKS - BEAT_JITTER_TICKS)`; no beat is suppressed in the final gap before `breach_due_T` (a beat whose nominal offset is below `tell_len` but within `BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS` of it is still published); and an un-aborted 1,800-tick tell with the level pinned at or above the hold line publishes exactly 19 beats with every jitter forced to +`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS`, 20 with jitter 0 and 23 with every jitter forced to −`BEAT_JITTER_TICKS` (integer model at the placeholders; closed form 20.6), and between 19 and 23 under the real hash *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the earlier band 19 to 22 failed the −8 case)*. [AT-9, F-12]
- **AC-AT-22 (auto) G4 save-equivalence.** GIVEN snapshots at the tick before TELL entry, mid-TELL, at the due run (pre and post), mid-BREACH, mid-SUBSIDING and in the refractory, WHEN each is restored and run forward with the same `restore_epoch` injected, THEN `state_hash` equals uninterrupted play at every tick and no cue fires on the restore itself; a different epoch changes only the tell lengths drawn after the restore *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. [AT-1, AT-27, AT-28, E-45 to E-47]
- **AC-AT-23 (auto) No reroll.** GIVEN a restore mid-TELL, WHEN the tell completes, THEN `rng_counter` advanced exactly once for that epoch and, restored with the same `restore_epoch`, the next organic tell's length equals the uninterrupted run's; with a different epoch it differs (AC-AT-130) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. [AT-1, AT-8]
- **AC-AT-24 (auto) Audio-first by construction.** GIVEN a TELL entry, WHEN the entry run completes, THEN `tell_beat` 0 and `phase` TELL are published by the same run, and no filament-resolve event for that epoch is emitted earlier. [AT-9, F-12]
- **AC-AT-82 (auto) Two crossings, one tell.** GIVEN two `LOUD` pushes in one run that crosses `theta` and a `LOUD` in the entry run, WHEN the run executes, THEN `tell_epoch` increases by exactly 1, `rng_counter` by exactly 1, `tell_beat` 0 is published in that run, `tell_len` is in `[TELL_DRAW_LO, TELL_DRAW_HI]` and no abort check runs on the entry run. [E-12, E-13, F-10]
- **AC-AT-25 (auto) The abort path.** GIVEN a TELL and `L` held under `theta_HOLD` for `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` (20 runs, the first run under counting as 1), WHEN the 20th run executes, THEN `phase` is CALM, `tell_outcome` is ABORTED, no STRESS is deposited, no `set_beacon` is submitted, `tell_beat` is frozen, `L` is unchanged and `rearm_until_T = T + TELL_REARM_TICKS`. [AT-10, F-14]
- **AC-AT-83 (auto) A dip resets the abort counter.** GIVEN a TELL, WHEN `L` is under `theta_HOLD` for 19 consecutive runs, at or above it for 1, then under it for 19 again, THEN no early abort occurs, the at-due decision still applies, and `below_hold_runs` reads 0 after the recovery run. [E-17, F-14]
- **AC-AT-26 (auto) The abort at the due run.** GIVEN `T >= breach_due_T` and `L' < theta_HOLD` (including the case where the early-abort counter also reaches its limit on that run), WHEN the run executes, THEN the result is one transition to CALM with ABORTED, never BREACH; at `L' == theta_HOLD` the breach commits. [AT-10, E-11, E-16]
- **AC-AT-84 (auto + data-lint) Abort relief.** GIVEN `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` 1000, WHEN a tell aborts, THEN `L` is unchanged (AC-AT-25); GIVEN 700, THEN `L' = min(L, B + 700 x (theta - B) div 1000)`; 600 is the lowest accepted value and 599 gives the advisory CI diff only (600 is guidance; no schema bound or named invariant binds it, so boot does not refuse and this is not a negative fixture) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix, round 4)*. [AT-10, F-14, Tuning Knobs]
- **AC-AT-27 (auto) Rearm.** GIVEN an abort at `T`, WHEN `L` returns above `theta` before `T + TELL_REARM_TICKS`, THEN no TELL starts until the first run at or after that tick, and the TELL then has `tell_elapsed` 0. [AT-10, E-14]
- **AC-AT-137 (auto) The rearm keys on the item.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* GIVEN a TELL during which a seat holds salvage item X (so X is in `tell_carry_items`), the seat drops X and the tell aborts at `T`, WHEN the seat takes X up again at `T + 120` with `L ≥ theta`, THEN on the first run that drains its `BURDEN` push `rearm_until_T` becomes that run's `T` and a TELL opens on the same run (all other guards open); GIVEN instead a different salvage item Y taken up at `T + 120`, THEN the rearm holds until `T + TELL_REARM_TICKS`; GIVEN a module picked up on the home ship, THEN no burden is pushed and the rearm holds; `tell_carry_items` is empty again once the rearm ends, and it survives a snapshot and restore bit-identically. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* (iv) GIVEN a seat holding salvage X at a non-home locus with X in `tell_carry_items`, the seat hops home and the due run `T_d` voids the tell (the only recorded occupant gone), WHEN CALM runs follow in the pod and aboard the home body with X still held, THEN no `BURDEN` push is drained, `rearm_until_T` stays `T_d` + 600, no TELL opens before it even with `L ≥ theta`, and the decay is not suspended. (v) GIVEN the same seat returns aboard the non-home body holding X at tick a, fixture only: hop at `t_hop` with d = 1,500 ticks of tell remaining, so `T_d` = `t_hop` + 1,500, `rearm_until_T` = `t_hop` + 2,100 and a = `t_hop` + 1,890 (+ at most 5 run-quantisation ticks) < `t_hop` + 2,100, THEN the first run that drains the `BURDEN` push sets `rearm_until_T` to that run's `T`; with d = 1,200 (a = `t_hop` + 1,890 ≥ `T_d` + 600 = `t_hop` + 1,800) the rearm has already ended and `tell_carry_items` is empty. [AT-3, AT-10, F-14, R5]
- **AC-AT-52 (data-lint) Rearm and refractory against the retry.** GIVEN the tell and breach data, WHEN loaded, THEN `TELL_REARM_TICKS >= AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS` and `REFRACT_TICKS >= AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS`. Negative fixture: `TELL_REARM_TICKS` 200. [AT-10, AT-29, F-21]
- **AC-AT-38 (auto) `tell_remaining`.** GIVEN any epoch, WHEN sampled every run, THEN it lies in `[0, TELL_MAX_TICKS]`, is 0 outside TELL and is non-increasing within an epoch. [AT-16, F-11]
- **AC-AT-69 (auto) Tier.** GIVEN `tell_elapsed` (the escalation clock) 594 and 600, WHEN runs execute, THEN `tier` is 1 and 2, and a clock frozen at 594 keeps tier 1 for the rest of the epoch (AC-AT-127); outside TELL it is 0; the whole floor window is tier 1. (Tier 3 from 1200 is AC-AT-81.) [AT-9, F-13]
- **AC-AT-91 (auto) Published fields by phase.** GIVEN a replay passing through CALM, TELL, BREACH, SUBSIDING and an abort, WHEN `attention.view` is sampled every run, THEN every field in the States and Transitions table (`tell_active`, `breach_phase` reporting TELL as CALM, `tell_remaining`, `tell_elapsed`, `tier`, `tell_beat`, `exogenous_permitted`, the commanded beacon, `tell_outcome` NONE / ABORTED / BREACHED with its persistence rules, `tell_epoch` +1 per entry) equals the table's row for that phase. [AT-16]
- **AC-AT-116 (auto) The act-to-tell chain.** GIVEN a `LOUD` push that takes `L` across `theta` in tick T with all four guards open, WHEN the sim runs, THEN TELL is entered at the first run at or after T (at most 6 ticks later, or 12 if the push landed after the hook in its tick) with `tell_beat` 0 published in that same run. [AT-3, AT-9, Game Feel]
- **AC-AT-47 (manual, Visual/Feel; deferred: audio half blocked on Adaptive Audio & Tells; visual half provisional on the Presenter) Audio is sufficient alone, and the visual channel is sufficient alone while an eligible emitter is lit.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a 600-tick armed tell with the beam on in a lit region, WHEN captured once with the audio muted and once audio-only (no video), THEN the muted capture shows at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` filament-resolve events inside the cone in the first 600 ticks and the audio-only capture has at least that many audible beats, where a beat counts as audible when its transient clears the concurrent non-tell content by at least `BEAT_TRANSIENT_MARGIN_DB` (Audio's value; momentary 400 ms loudness, the AC-AT-122 method) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the transient margin, not the bed's `TELL_AUDIBILITY_MARGIN_DB`)*; NOT ASSESSED until Audio's assets and the reference scene exist (owner: audio-director; gate: the Audio vertical-slice spike). The headless half is AC-AT-20. Both captures retained in `production/qa/evidence/attention/` with the art-director's sign-off. [AT-9, AT-24, ART:954]

### E. The breach

- **AC-AT-28 (auto) Breach entry.** GIVEN a TELL due run with `L' >= theta_HOLD` and a locus body of S sections, WHEN the run executes, THEN `phase` is BREACH, `breach_enter_T = T`, M17 `deposit_trace(STRESS, s, magnitude = BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT)` is called for each section in ascending `section_id`, at most `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` per run, the whole body is covered within `ceil(S / STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN)` runs, and the magnitude argument is the registry constant (not a literal). [AT-11, F-18]
- **AC-AT-29 (auto) The beacon command.** GIVEN BREACH entry, WHEN the next tick applies, THEN `set_beacon(on)` is submitted as a SYSTEM command at T+1 with `max_age_ticks` 120 (for a derelict locus too: the one home-ship beacon); at SUBSIDING entry `set_beacon(off)` is submitted. GIVEN each rejection reason `NOT_LIVE`, `NO_SUCH_EMITTER` and `NOT_BEACON`, THEN the machine still advances, the failure is logged once and the command is retried once per run for at most `BEACON_RETRY_RUNS`. [AT-11, AT-13, AT-18, E-30]
- **AC-AT-58 (auto) Breach teeth, seeded fault.** GIVEN a golden file produced by running the Graph alone, with and without STRESS, on a locus body seeded with one active overload (a cable) and one fatigued plate, WHEN the BREACH runs its full `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, THEN the `hp` loss on each seeded subject with STRESS equals the golden STRESS value exactly and the ratio to the no-STRESS value is `BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT / 1000` (2.0) within an integer tolerance of one hp unit per subject per armed step (the Graph owner confirms the tolerance). The clean-ship companion stays labelled contract-only (it tests the tell, not the teeth) and is not a model of an MVP derelict: the content requirement is AC-AT-117. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The integer tolerance is the Graph owner's to confirm (OQ-AT-6); until then this AC is not story-ready. [AT-11, E-35]
- **AC-AT-117 (data-lint, authoring; Derelict Authoring is designed, so the lint runs now that OQ-AT-24 is answered by the user ruling) Every possible locus body has teeth.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the MVP derelict A and derelict B data and every body that can be a locus (each derelict alone and each welded to the home-ship), WHEN the authoring lint runs, THEN each such body contains at least one Graph subject authored active with an overload (a cable) or a fatigue fault (a plate), the same state the AC-AT-58 seeded scenario uses; a body with none fails with the named check `DERELICT_LOCUS_BODY_NO_FAULT` (Derelict Authoring's name, adopted), whose severity and stage are Derelict Authoring's (WARN at CI; ERROR now that the user has ruled option (b), OQ-DA-30 recording it); Attention requests no seventh BOOT rule beyond DA-41's six. The pass condition for derelict A is option (b) (OQ-AT-24, **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions)**): a Graph subject authored with a fault that wakes when its bus is served counts (A: `j_a_main` once its bus is served, with `pump_a` on the branch). **Written exemption list:** (E1) the home-ship body (its faults are the crew's to repair); (E2) the pod body (dormant always and never a locus, WM-5); no other body is exempt, a body with none fails `DERELICT_LOCUS_BODY_NO_FAULT`, and Derelict Authoring's alternative exemption for a dead-bus derelict is **not** adopted here. This turns AC-AT-58's seeded fault into a content requirement. [AT-11, E-35, AC-AT-58]
- **AC-AT-31 (auto) STRESS never lapses mid-BREACH.** GIVEN a full-length BREACH, WHEN the Graph's STRESS armed state is sampled every run (through the Graph's named read, or a test-only accessor until the Graph owner names one; see Gaps), THEN every section of the body is armed at every run after its first deposit, and the coverage margin `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS - STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS - STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS` is positive. [AT-12, F-18]
- **AC-AT-32 (auto) Every end clause.** GIVEN BREACH, WHEN each of the following is driven in turn, THEN the machine moves to SUBSIDING: `D >= BREACH_MAX_TICKS` (3,600); `D >= BREACH_MIN_TICKS` and 50 consecutive runs under `theta_HOLD` (earliest at D = 1,194); the last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry leaving a non-home `locus_body`; an empty non-home locus body at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` (evaluated at entry only). GIVEN both end clauses true on one run, THEN there is one transition and the deposits stop. [AT-12, F-17, E-18, E-33]
- **AC-AT-33 (data-lint) The refresh fits inside the hold.** GIVEN the breach data and the cited `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS`, WHEN loaded, THEN `STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS + STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS < STRESS_HOLD_STEPS` and `STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS x STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN >= S_max` (24 >= 14). Negative fixture: `STRESS_REFRESH_RUNS` 598. [AT-12, F-18]
- **AC-AT-93 (auto) The burst schedule.** GIVEN a 5-section body and `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, WHEN the BREACH runs, THEN deposits occur at run q = 0, then q = 300, 301 and 302, then at q = 600 only if the breach is still running, with no deposit on the run that ends it. GIVEN a fixture with `STRESS_REFRESH_BURST_RUNS` 1, `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 8 and a body of 9 sections (above the burst capacity of 8), THEN the second F-18 lint fails at authoring and, if the data is forced through, the cursor wraps and the remaining section waits for the next burst. [AT-12, F-18, E-37]
- **AC-AT-34 (auto) The level keeps integrating.** GIVEN BREACH with the player running, WHEN runs execute, THEN `L` keeps rising, the breach does not end before `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, and a quiet player ends it at the earliest at D = 1,194. [AT-12]
- **AC-AT-35 (auto) The subside reset (entry).** GIVEN SUBSIDING entry with `L` above the residual, WHEN it executes, THEN `L' = min(L, B + POST_PM x (theta - B) div 1000)` (2,580,000 at `B` 300,000), `set_beacon(off)` is submitted and no further STRESS is deposited; GIVEN `L` below the residual at entry, THEN `L` is unchanged. [AT-13, F-19]
- **AC-AT-98 (auto) SUBSIDING and its end.** GIVEN SUBSIDING entry at T, WHEN `T + SUBSIDE_TICKS` is reached, THEN the first run at or after it returns to CALM, sets `tell_outcome` BREACHED and `refract_until_T = T_end + REFRACT_TICKS`; `exogenous_permitted` is true until that run. [AT-13, F-19]
- **AC-AT-36 (auto) The refractory.** GIVEN the end of SUBSIDING, WHEN `L >= theta` during `REFRACT_TICKS`, THEN no TELL starts before `refract_until_T`, the level keeps integrating, and the first legal run past it enters TELL. [AT-13, E-14]
- **AC-AT-60 (auto) The transition table.** GIVEN the state table, WHEN every ordered pair of states is attempted, THEN only CALM to TELL, TELL to CALM (three guards: the abort hold, the at-due check and the departure void *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*), TELL to BREACH, BREACH to SUBSIDING and SUBSIDING to CALM occur, CALM to BREACH, TELL to SUBSIDING and BREACH to TELL never do, and `phase` changes only inside `attention.step`. [AT-13, States and Transitions]
- **AC-AT-37 (auto) The locus.** GIVEN ledgers {A 3,000,000; B 3,000,000; home 1,400,000} at TELL entry, WHEN the locus is chosen, THEN it is the lower `section_id` of A and B; given the locus section cut away mid-event (not a departure, which voids instead, AC-AT-46), THEN it falls back to the home-ship's lowest `section_id`, `locus_body` is recomputed and `tell_remaining` is unchanged. [AT-14, F-6, E-27, E-28]
- **AC-AT-87 (auto) The ledger and the fixed locus.** GIVEN `ledger[A]` 3,000,000 and `I_s` 30,324, WHEN a run executes, THEN `ledger'[A]` is 3,020,324 and the array stays sorted by `section_id`; a ledger that decays to 0 persists as 0; once a TELL has fixed the locus, a later change of ledger order does not move it before the epoch ends. [AT-14, F-6, E-28]
- **AC-AT-59 (auto) The decoy knob.** GIVEN `LOCUS_OCC_PREF_PM` 1000, WHEN a lit unoccupied derelict has the higher ledger, THEN the locus is that derelict exactly as AT-14 states; with a lower value the occupied section can win. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* Light and power feed the ledger (F-2, F-6); two fixtures, each run to steady state at `tau` 1,800 before TELL entry: **(i) walk with the beam in A while home is lit** (A's ledger 2,318,400, home 1,379,000) gives locus A; **(ii) weld at home while A's two fixtures burn and A stays loaded** (home clamped at 6,120,000, A 518,400) gives locus home; and the companion **(iii) stand still with the beam in A while home is lit** (A 518,400, home 1,379,000) gives locus home. A build that drops the light or power term from `I_s` fails (iii). [AT-14, F-6, Moment C]
- **AC-AT-46 (auto; the derivable half runs now against Ship Body's `actor_section`, the departure half runs against `web.state`'s read view (`pod_at` / `transit_state` / `leg_epoch`) read in the Attention hook; `POD_DEPARTED` is a presentation (Audio) event only *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0006 sync)*) Pod-hop clauses.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *Clause 1 (the void):* GIVEN a non-home locus body whose only occupant at TELL entry (`tell_occupants_at_entry`) hops away during the TELL, WHEN the due run executes, THEN `phase` is CALM, `tell_outcome` is ABORTED, no STRESS is deposited, no beacon command is submitted, `rearm_until_T` is set, the departed section's dormancy at run N and the void at the due run N+k produce no `LOCUS_FALLBACK` at any run between, `locus_section_id` stays pinned to the departed body, and `TellAudit` records no harm event on the home ship. GIVEN two occupants at entry and one leaves, THEN the breach proceeds on the locus body. GIVEN a home-ship locus whose occupant hops away, THEN the due run breaches (the void does not apply). *Clause 2 (X10, reachable only through a berthed derelict at MVP; the departed-derelict form is fixture-only):* GIVEN a lit non-home locus body with no living seat at TELL entry that Web Map keeps loaded, THEN BREACH does not end by clause (iii) and ends at `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` by X10. *Clause 3 (BREACH departure):* GIVEN a non-home `locus_body` whose last living occupant recorded at BREACH entry (`locus_occupants_at_entry`) leaves it during BREACH, THEN BREACH ends at the next run; GIVEN two occupants at BREACH entry and one leaves, THEN BREACH continues; a departure from a home-ship locus does not end BREACH. A pod hop during TELL never interrupts the tell itself. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *Clause 1 fixtures (the `occ_gone` rule, F-14):* GIVEN `actor_section` returning NONE for the only recorded occupant during the TELL (and, separately, during the BREACH), THEN the void does not fire at the due run and clause (iii) does not end the breach, and one diagnostic is logged per epoch; GIVEN the only recorded occupant of a two-seat crew dying inside the locus body while the other seat (never recorded) is elsewhere, THEN the void does not fire; GIVEN a recorded occupant who goes `IN_POD` and then dies there, THEN it counts as gone. *Clause 4 (E-73, home-ship locus with nobody aboard):* GIVEN a home-ship locus with `tell_occupants_at_entry` and `locus_occupants_at_entry` both empty, THEN the due run breaches on the level alone, `set_beacon(on)` is submitted at T+1, STRESS is deposited on the home body, `TellAudit` reports 0 violations, clauses (iii) and (iv) never fire and the breach ends only by `BREACH_MAX_TICKS` or by the clear-hold rule. **Story-readiness:** the gate with Web Map's AC-WM-122 is lifted (2026-10-28); AC-WM-105 now asserts the same pinned-locus and void behaviour as clause 1 *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. [AT-22, E-23, E-62, E-69, E-73, F-14]
- **AC-AT-42 (auto) The beacon within a run.** GIVEN BREACH entry with a live home-ship beacon, WHEN two ticks elapse, THEN the beacon emitter is `lit` and the emitter list shows it; the world holds exactly one BEACON (`BEACON_COUNT`). [AT-18, DA-24]
- **AC-AT-65 (auto) Post-restore beacon reconciliation.** GIVEN a snapshot taken in BREACH with the SYSTEM command dropped on reload (beacon off, `beacon_commanded` on), WHEN restored, THEN `post_restore` changes no `phase` and submits no command, and the first `attention.step` run submits `set_beacon(on)` exactly once; with the states already matching, no command is submitted. [AT-27, E-48]
- **AC-AT-92 (auto) A dropped STRESS deposit.** GIVEN a Horror double that saturates `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` before Attention's P3 call, WHEN a BREACH enters, THEN the dropped sections are unstressed, `TellAudit` records no violation and the next burst re-arms them; with the double's budget at `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP - STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` all eight deposits are honoured. [AT-11, E-29, C22]
- **AC-AT-94 (auto) The STRESS tail.** GIVEN a breach that ends at D = 1,194 and one that ends at 3,600, WHEN SUBSIDING ends, THEN the Graph's STRESS is still armed for 1,206 ticks and for 612 ticks respectively, Attention made no deposit after the ending run, `phase` reads CALM, and Attention's published surfaces contain no all-clear mapping. [E-34, F-18]
- **AC-AT-94b (integration, auto) Death restore.** GIVEN every seat DEAD mid-TELL and the Save/Load death stub restoring the newest verified autosave, WHEN the same inputs replay, THEN `rng_counter`, `tell_len`, `breach_due_T` and the beat sequence of a tell already drawn in the snapshot are identical to the first run and no onset cue fires on the restore, while the next organic tell drawn after the restore uses the incremented `restore_epoch` and differs in length from the dead run's; the Graph's failures replay exactly (user ruling D16) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. This is the MVP death handling the user chose. [E-22, AT-27]

### F. View, truth channel, presentation

- **AC-AT-39 (source-lint) Reader scope.** GIVEN the readers of `attention.view`, WHEN scanned, THEN no PRESENTATION subscriber reads `tell_remaining`, `tell_progress_pm` does not exist, and `tell_remaining` is read only by Save/Load, SIM readers and the DIAGNOSTIC Debug Overlay. Negative fixture: a presenter stub that reads `tell_remaining`. [AT-16]
- **AC-AT-67 (source-lint) `phase`, not `breach_phase`.** GIVEN the Adaptive Audio subscriber (when written) and any double, WHEN scanned, THEN it reads `phase` and never `breach_phase`; and `breach_phase` reports TELL as CALM. [AT-16, C5]
- **AC-AT-40 (auto + source-lint) No `PERCEPTION_OUT`.** GIVEN the registry entry for each `attention.view` field and for the view builder, WHEN its source blocks are listed, THEN every source block has `class SIM` and none is `PERCEPTION_OUT`; the builder's source contains no read of a `PERCEPTION_OUT` block id; and the field set equals exactly the AT-16 table (adding a field fails the test). [AT-16, AT-17, AC-SL-95a, AC-SL-121]
- **AC-AT-41 (source-lint) The truth channel.** GIVEN the Perception Filter's write set and Sanity's, WHEN scanned, THEN neither contains an Attention cue (a filament, a bed beat, a beacon state), the Filter's anchor list includes the tell cues, and Attention reads no sanity value. [AT-17, E-55]
- **AC-AT-66 (source-lint + review) No meter anywhere.** GIVEN the UI and presentation source roots (HUD, Chart Table and menus, In-World Instruments, Settings, Onboarding) and `assets/`, with the allow-list `{Debug Overlay root, diagnostics channel, tests/}` in the lint config, WHEN scanned for the tokens `level_pm`, `level_mAU`, `tell_remaining` and `attention_level`, THEN there are no hits outside the allow-list; the AT-16 named readers outside those roots (the Presenter and Audio for quantised `level_pm`; Save/Load and Horror for `tell_remaining`) and Attention's own module (`level_mAU`) are out of the scan's scope by construction, mirroring Debug Overlay AC-DO-17 (OQ-DO-22); and GIVEN the review checklist (the accessibility settings screen and each HUD spec have no field that displays or sonifies the level; no caption, vignette or glyph), THEN every box is ticked; the Debug Overlay is absent from release builds; and GIVEN the release export's file list, THEN no path under `overlay_source_root` and no name in `dev_artifact_list` appears (registry `overlay_build_gate`). [UI Requirements, AT-26, ART:741, :954]
- **AC-AT-103 (source-lint) No captions.** GIVEN the project, WHEN scanned, THEN no caption field or caption string exists for tell beats, and `attention.view` carries no field named or typed as displayed text. [AT-24, ART:741]
- **AC-AT-129 (auto + manual) The beat haptic pulse is opt-in and carries beats only.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN the setting off (the default), THEN no haptic call is made in any phase; GIVEN it on and a replay through CALM, a TELL that aborts and a TELL that breaches, THEN exactly one pulse fires per `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` change, at one fixed amplitude and duration, dispatched on the same notification as the audio request; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* the pulse lasts `HAPTIC_PULSE_MS` (`[PLACEHOLDER]` 60 ms, a presenter value in `presentation.json`, not a sim constant) and its measured onset at the device lies within one rendered frame (17 ms) of the audio beat's audible onset, measured with the output-latency method of AC-AT-123 (the haptic path gets the same latency compensation as the visual lead-in, and the AC is NOT ASSESSED until that spike exists); none fires in CALM, at a pre-tell creak, at the release, at the alarm or the beacon, on a load or a pause, and at most one fires after a hitch; a scan finds no haptic call keyed to `level_pm`, `tier`, `phase` or the cue step; the manual half is a deaf or muted tester in the dark reporting a beat count within one of the audio's, retained with the accessibility-specialist's sign-off. [AT-24, AUD-2, E-72]
- **AC-AT-43 (review + source-lint) The suppressant contract.** GIVEN the Suit and Attention contract text, WHEN reviewed, THEN `tool_charge(player_id, tool_suppressant)`, `consume_charge(player_id, def_id) -> ok` and `OUT_OF_CHARGES` are the only names, no effect verb ships at MVP, and the wording is "repel or distract"; and `tool_suppressant` appears in no MVP start-kit list (X6, user ruling) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. [AT-19]
- **AC-AT-44 (manual, Visual/Feel, screenshots at every zoom rung) Filaments only in lit regions of eligible emitters.** GIVEN a tell, WHEN captured at each integer zoom rung `k` in `[1, k_auto + 2]` in five scenes (beam on; beam off with a FIXTURE lit; beam off with a NODE_LANTERN or DEPLOYABLE lit; only a BEACON, `fx_cryo_lamp`, a DOCK_STATUS glow or a FAULT_GLOW lit; nothing lit), THEN filaments appear only inside the lit region of a BEAM, FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN or DEPLOYABLE and never in unlit space; none is drawn in the excluded-emitters-only scene or the nothing-lit scene; filaments are 1 art px wide without anti-aliasing smear at the tips, and a resolved strand still reads at the zoom-out rung (trunk at least 2 px, the 50 percent scale test of ART 5.6). Captures named `AC-AT-44-k[rung]-[scene]-[date].png`, art-director sign-off. If the producer takes the cone-only scope fallback, the fixture and lantern scenes are expected to show none and the record says so. [AT-20, ART:302, AV-13, AV-14]
- **AC-AT-45 (data-lint + manual) The palette.** GIVEN the presenter's data and code, WHEN scanned, THEN every colour literal is a member of the art bible's iridescence list at ART:1371 (copied into the test data with its source line); GIVEN the retained engine screenshots of AC-AT-44 and AC-AT-111 under Tungsten, Chemlight and the beacon sweep, WHEN every strand pixel is sampled, THEN none falls in the violet arc (hue 270 to 330) or the flesh range (hue 5 to 30, S 20 to 60 percent, V 45 to 85 percent), the band peak is at or under `BAND_PEAK_LSTAR_MAX` and no bloom appears at the glow threshold. [AT-20, ART:1371, Palette rules]
- **AC-AT-53 (auto) The camera does nothing.** GIVEN an identical input trace, WHEN played through CALM, TELL and BREACH, THEN the camera transform and zoom, the beam's `output_pm` and `reach_px`, the global darkness level and the input map are byte-identical across the three; the only lighting changes allowed are Lighting's own tier-driven lamp gutter on FIXTURE and NODE_LANTERN and the BEACON emitter, each checked against Lighting's criteria. [AT-20, PIPE:151, AV-1]
- **AC-AT-49a (auto) Quantised cues.** GIVEN `level_pm` swept from 0 to 1000 and back with 20-permille jitter around each edge, WHEN the cue step is computed, THEN it takes only the values 0 to `CUE_STEPS`, rises at the edges 800, 850, 900 and 950, falls only at 775, 825, 875 and 925 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, produces no cue change faster than one per edge crossing, and pre-tell cue events are absent from `TellAudit` and from the floor-window beat count. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN a TELL whose level decays from 1000 through every rung to below the floor before it aborts, THEN `cue_step` reads 0 on every TELL run and no pre-tell cue event is requested; GIVEN the first CALM run after that abort with `level_pm` at 900 (above two rise edges), THEN `cue_step` is 0 and no cue event is requested on that run, and the step rises to 1 only when `level_pm` next crosses 800 from below; the same holds on the first CALM run after SUBSIDING. [AT-26, F-24]
- **AC-AT-49b (data-lint) The cue-floor calibration.** GIVEN the presentation data, WHEN loaded, THEN `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM >= max over rings of walk_beam_pm + CUE_MARGIN_PM` (671 at the MVP ring plus 100 gives 771, so 800 passes; the superseded 350 and 550 fail; the ring-3 test row needs 850, which fails the rung lint `(1000 − floor) div CUE_STEPS ≥ 50`, so it is flagged `SWITCHES`). Negative fixtures: 350 and 550. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* [AT-26, F-24, C23]
- **AC-AT-49c (auto) No continuous mapping.** GIVEN `level_pm` swept across the interior of one cue step, WHEN the presenter's output state is compared, THEN it is byte-identical (no density, size, brightness, band intensity or dust amplitude changes), because the token scan of AC-AT-66 cannot see an indirect mapping. [AT-26, AV-6]
- **AC-AT-50 (auto) Cues fire on transitions only.** GIVEN a save made in CALM with ABORTED, in TELL, in BREACH and in SUBSIDING, WHEN each is loaded, THEN no onset, breach-enter, release or subside cue fires on the load. [AT-27, E-49]
- **AC-AT-102 (manual, Visual/Feel) Accessibility and the beam.** GIVEN the reduce-motion and reduce-flashing settings, WHEN a tell is captured, THEN filament motion becomes a density change (the per-beat density step still rises), the beacon becomes the static lit lens, the dust turns in the beam and no hull-stress hairline is drawn; retained as `AC-AT-102-[setting]-[date].png` in `production/qa/evidence/attention/` with art-director sign-off. [AT-20, AT-24, AV-14, Accessibility and reduced-effect behaviour]
- **AC-AT-107 (auto, presenter double) Guaranteed resolve.** GIVEN a lit region with at least one unresolved eligible site, WHEN each beat publishes, THEN at least one strand resolves in that cone; a cone with no eligible site on screen is the only exception and the harness logs it; and the visual resolve count per lit region in the first `TELL_MIN_TICKS` equals the published beat count (the visual half of AC-AT-20). [AV-7]
- **AC-AT-118 (auto, presenter double) Strand eligibility by emitter kind.** GIVEN a presenter double and one emitter of each kind (`BEAM`, `FIXTURE`, `NODE_LANTERN`, `DEPLOYABLE`, `BEACON`, `FAULT_GLOW`) plus the `fx_cryo_lamp` EMERGENCY fixture and a DOCK_STATUS fixture, WHEN site eligibility is evaluated at a site in each lit region and in unlit space, THEN it is true for the first four, false for `BEACON`, `FAULT_GLOW`, `fx_cryo_lamp` and the DOCK_STATUS glow, and false in unlit space *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; a site lit by both an eligible and an excluded emitter is eligible through the eligible one only. [AT-20, AV-7, AV-14, LO-20]
- **AC-AT-108 (manual, Visual/Feel) Ring separation.** GIVEN a ring-3 CALM frame sequence and a ring-3 TELL tier-1 sequence with brightness equalised, WHEN shown to 8 testers, THEN at least 7 of 8 tell them apart by shape and motion alone *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (chance is 4 of 8); retained captures with art-director sign-off. [AV-16]
- **AC-AT-109 (manual, Visual/Feel; Sanity & Hallucination Rules is designed, the fringe asset is the remaining blocker) Rim contention.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a strand and a stage-2 violet fringe on the same rim, WHEN compared hue-removed, in a single still frame and motion-frozen, THEN each comparison is classified correctly by at least 7 of 8 testers (chance is 4 of 8) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, and a second client shows the strands and not the fringe. A fringe that cannot be tangential, continuous, breathing and per-viewer is a CONFLICT for the creative-director. [AV-17, cone-rim contention]
- **AC-AT-110 (source-lint + auto) Not an emitter.** GIVEN the presenter, WHEN scanned, THEN no presenter output is registered as an emitter or appears in `emitters()`, and no Attention input reads a presenter value; GIVEN a replay with the presenter on and off, THEN `I_light` is identical. [AV-15]
- **AC-AT-111 (manual, Visual/Feel) Release is not a beat.** GIVEN captures of an abort, of a void (a hop away from a derelict locus during a TELL) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* and of SUBSIDING, THEN the retractions contain no band-slide, BREACH entry shows the snap and then no motion at the rim, and no success or all-clear visual appears; retained with art-director sign-off. [AV-9, AV-10, AV-11]
- **AC-AT-112 (auto + manual) Restore, pause, hitch (visual).** GIVEN the first rendered frame after a restore in each phase, THEN the correct end state shows with no transition animation; GIVEN a stall of several beats, THEN at most one beat plays and none is replayed. [AV-12]
- **AC-AT-113 (source-lint) Exclusive assets.** GIVEN the project, WHEN scanned, THEN no consumer other than the Attention Presenter references `web_strand_*`, `vfx_tell_*` or the band-slide animation, and no Sanity, Horror or Perception Filter asset tag shares the family. [AV-18]
- **AC-AT-114 (manual, Visual/Feel) Readability.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a resolved strand crossing a failing pipe or a trail, THEN it never out-contrasts it (the ART 3.5 trail-contrast test, once its number exists; deferred until then), and neither `STRAND_VIEW_CAP` (9 per lit region) nor `STRAND_SCREEN_CAP` (16 across the screen) is exceeded in any retained capture or in a 10-minute soak with four lit regions, counted by the presenter double (0 frames over the cap). [AV-8]
- **AC-AT-121 (manual, Visual/Feel; deferred: needs Audio's creak and beat assets) Creak versus beat.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN 8 first-time listeners with no briefing and 20 randomised 5-second clips from the named reference scene (10 pre-tell creaks across steps 1 to `CUE_STEPS`, 10 tell beats across tiers 1 to 3), WHEN each marks every clip "ordinary ship creak" or "it noticed", THEN overall accuracy is at least 90 percent and no listener is below 80 percent; a result below that sends the creak and beat families back to Audio (disjoint identity, AUD-1, AUD-8). The creative-director accepts that quantised creaks are a **semi-indicator for hearing players** (AT-26); recorded here and in OQ-AT-1. [AT-26, AUD-1, AUD-8]
- **AC-AT-136 (manual, Feel; deferred: Audio's beat assets) The freeze is audible.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN 8 listeners with no briefing and paired 10-second clips from the named reference scene, one with the escalation clock frozen at `e` 600 and one with it running from 600 (the AC-AT-135 schedules, real jitter), presented in random order, WHEN each marks the clip in which "the creaking is speeding up", THEN at least 7 of 8 pick the running clip (chance is 4 of 8). Below that, the freeze is inaudible against the jitter, and the options go to the user (a falling-level freeze key, a published 'held' fact for Audio, or smaller jitter); OQ-AT-25 is not reopened by this result alone. Owner: audio-director. [AT-9, F-12, F-13, AUD-5]
- **AC-AT-122 (manual, Visual/Feel; deferred: Audio's reference scene and spike) Bed and transient audibility, measured.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The 6 dB margin has a method *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: K-weighted loudness has no per-band form, so the bed is measured as per-band RMS)*: separate margins for the **bed** (RMS level in dB in each of M2's two bands, the low structure-borne band and the upper-partial edge layer, over a 10 s window, each band at least `TELL_AUDIBILITY_MARGIN_DB` above the loudest concurrent non-tell content in the same band, including the player's own `LOUD` act at beat 0) and for **beat transients** (momentary 400 ms loudness, at least `BEAT_TRANSIENT_MARGIN_DB`, a value Audio sets and owns; this GDD seeds none), against the loudest concurrent non-tell bed in the named reference scene `scene_audibility_worst` (Audio's D.6 worst set: running loops at their ceiling, one TOOL-class loop, ambience, Suit fan, berth bed, INTEGRITY tone and `WIT_VOICE_CAP` witness classes at the reference mix), captured on the reference output device and retained in `production/qa/evidence/attention/` with the audio-director's sign-off. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* The same capture checks M4: the breach-entry step (BS.1770 momentary maximum after the breach-enter event minus that over the last beat) is at most `BREACH_ENTRY_STEP_LU_MAX`. NOT ASSESSED until Audio's reference scene and spike exist (owner: audio-director). [M2, M4, AUD-4]
- **AC-AT-123 (auto + spike) Audio first against output latency.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the measured output latency `L_out_ms` on the reference device (OQ-AA-4), WHEN a beat publishes, THEN the first visual change for that beat appears no earlier than the audio's audible onset (`VISUAL_LEAD_IN_TICKS ≥ ceil(L_out_ms × 60 / 1000)`); until the spike exists the value is 0 and the AC is marked deferred, not passed. [AUD-2, AC-AT-24]
- **AC-AT-124 (auto, performance; budgets provisional: the technical-director confirms) Presenter frame cost.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN `STRAND_SCREEN_CAP` (16) resolved strands in four lit regions with dust impulses, WHEN the presenter runs 10,000 render frames on each reference machine, RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (a release export, `qa.min_spec`, delegated ruling R12) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*, THEN its p99 cost is at most 0.25 ms (a placeholder) and no frame exceeds 1 ms; the simulation budget of AC-AT-54b (0.05 ms) is confirmed by the same technical-director pass; pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK. [AV-8, STRAND_SCREEN_CAP, AC-AT-54b]
- **AC-AT-115 (auto) The dedupe pair.** GIVEN a replay with an aborted tell followed by a second tell, WHEN `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)` is sampled each run, THEN the pair is strictly increasing lexicographically across the whole replay (the new epoch's beat 0 follows the old epoch's last beat); a `tell_beat` jump of N > 1 between polls is one observable change. The audio behaviour that consumes it is AC-AT-78. [AT-16, AUD-3]

### G. Scope, edges and determinism

- **AC-AT-48 (source-lint) One-way edges.** GIVEN the sources of the Graph, Controller, Suit, Interaction verb data, camera, Failure & Trail and Item DB, WHEN scanned, THEN none reads an Attention symbol; and Attention's source reads nothing of Lighting, Audio, Sanity, Horror, the Perception Filter, HUD, Instruments, Failure & Trail, Camera or Save/Load. Negative fixture: a Graph stub that reads `level_pm`. [AT-25]
- **AC-AT-55 (auto) Seat state.** GIVEN seats in states IDLE, DOWNED, DEAD and IN_POD, WHEN `B` and the pod hold are computed, THEN DOWNED counts as living, DEAD does not, and IN_POD holds the departure ring; with every seat DEAD `B` holds its last value. [AT-23, AT-31, E-21, E-63]
- **AC-AT-133 (auto) Death in the pod.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN a seat IN_POD whose `loco_state` becomes DEAD (a Controller double) while a TELL runs, WHEN runs execute, THEN it stops counting as living for `B` and the pod hold on the next run and the tell is unchanged; with another seat living at home `B` follows that seat, with every seat DEAD `B` holds its last value, and a recorded occupant of a non-home locus whose section already left `locus_body` is not counted as present. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN instead a recorded occupant that becomes DEAD while inside a non-home locus body, THEN its `occ_gone` stays false, the void does not fire at the due run, and during BREACH clause (iii) does not end the breach while it is the last recorded occupant. [AT-22, AT-31, E-69, F-14]
- **AC-AT-56 (auto) Diagnostics are inert.** GIVEN a replay with the diagnostic subscriber on and off, WHEN `state_hash` is compared at every tick, THEN it is identical and no sim path reads a diagnostic counter. [AT-32]
- **AC-AT-100 (auto) Diagnostics content.** GIVEN a replay with several tells, aborts and breaches, WHEN the diagnostic subscriber is read, THEN it holds the counts of entries, aborts and breaches, ticks per phase, the per-channel contribution before each TELL entry, abort latency and locus occupancy at breach, and AC-AT-80's abort share can be computed from it; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN one tell aborted by restraint and one voided by a hop, THEN both publish `tell_outcome` ABORTED and the diagnostic counts one restraint abort and one void. [AT-32]
- **AC-AT-51 (data-lint) The event-defer invariant.** GIVEN the cited and configured values, WHEN loaded, THEN `TELL_MAX_TICKS + BREACH_MAX_TICKS + SUBSIDE_TICKS + AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS < AUTOSAVE_MAX_DEFER_TICKS` (6,900 < 7,200), re-checked across `gate_tell_mode` FAIR and STRICT; negative fixture: `BREACH_MAX_TICKS` 3,900 fails. [AT-29, F-20]
- **AC-AT-91b (integration, auto) Save/Load gate.** GIVEN the real autosave gate, WHEN an autosave comes due during TELL, THEN the FAIR-mode window stays live because `breach_phase` is CALM, under both `gate_tell_mode` FAIR and STRICT; a save taken mid-TELL restores the full tell. [AT-16, E-51]
- **AC-AT-61 (review) MVP scope.** GIVEN the GDD, WHEN reviewed, THEN multiple tiers, per-ring thresholds, the suppressant verb, per-player attribution, rate-coupled length and co-op scaling appear only as named Vertical Slice addenda, the per-ring stimulus gain appears only as the pillar-flagged Vertical Slice requirement of AT-6, no rule absorbs any of them, and phantom tells and false calm appear nowhere as an addendum (rejected by pillar, AT-17). [AT-30]
- **AC-AT-70 (auto) Restore failures are hard.** GIVEN a block with a `schema_version` outside `supported_versions [1]`, an `L` above `L_CEIL`, an incoherent phase, an unknown section id, or an `rng_counter` greater than `tell_epoch` (armed tells draw nothing, so the counter never exceeds the epoch count), or *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* a `pending_noise_mAU` or `pending_ledger_mAU[s]` above `L_CEIL`, a pending-ledger, ledger or `nl_hist_w` key that is duplicated, unsorted or not a loaded section, more entries than loaded sections, an `nl_hist_w` value outside `[0, 10,000]`, or a `tell_occupants_at_entry` seat that is not a bound seat, or an `esc_ticks` above `TELL_MAX_TICKS`, not a multiple of 6, non-zero outside TELL or above `T − tell_start_T` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, WHEN restored, THEN restore reports `ctx.fault(ATTENTION_RESTORE_SCHEMA / _RANGE / _PHASE_INCOHERENT / _SECTION_UNKNOWN / _RNG_COUNTER)` with a named detail and returns no `(ok, reason, detail)` value, the kernel fires `load_failed` (mapped by Save/Load to the `RESTORE_FAILED` class) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0006 sync)*, nothing is clamped, and a restore round trip on a good block is idempotent. [AT-1, E-50]
- **AC-AT-97 (auto) Dirty keys.** GIVEN one run that changes `L` only and one that changes `phase`, WHEN the dirty set is read, THEN it is `{ATT/L}` and `{ATT/L, ATT/P}`; an element write into a sorted section array marks its key; the set never exceeds `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256. [AT-1]
- **AC-AT-71 (auto) Pause.** GIVEN a paused game, WHEN wall time passes, THEN sim `T` does not advance, Attention runs on `T` only and no catch-up beat or decay occurs on resume. [AT-28, E-52]
- **AC-AT-72 (auto; Modular Ship Assembly is designed, runs when its block exists) Weld double count.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN a weld completing in a derelict welded to the home ship, WHEN the run executes, THEN zero `LOUD` is pushed at the weld completion (by Interaction or Assembly), and the next breach's `locus_body` spans both bodies; GIVEN a seam-cut reaching DONE, THEN exactly one `LOUD` (Interaction's, IA-33) is pushed and Assembly pushes none. [AT-4, E-44]
- **AC-AT-73 (auto) Hallucination boundary.** GIVEN a Sanity double that raises a phantom entity, a false "hull failing" state, a phantom emitter and phantom loot while `phase` is CALM, WHEN `attention.view`, `emitters()` and `TellAudit` are read, THEN the view reports CALM, `TellAudit` holds no event, nothing is added to `emitters()` or to the pushed noise, and no Attention symbol changed. [AT-17, E-53, E-54, E-57]
- **AC-AT-101 (source-lint) Determinism hygiene.** GIVEN the Attention and presenter sources, WHEN scanned, THEN no float type or literal, `randf`, `randi`, wall-clock call or `Dictionary` iteration appears in sim code; the only RNG draw is `RngFacet.draw_epoch` for the tell, and no `DetRng.derive` or `rng_derive` call appears in presenter code; beat jitter and presenter randomness call `Ihash.h32` with a fixed non-zero `HASH_SEED` *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0005 sync)*. [AT-28, AT-20, F-12]
- **AC-AT-54a (spike; a story-readiness gate: it runs before any story for AT-5, AT-28 or F-5 is written) Engine arithmetic and storage.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Integer-division semantics are closed by ADR-0005 s1.4 and removed from this spike: formulas use `IntMath.floor_div` / `ceil_div` / `posmod_i`, and the lint forbids bare `/` and `%` (ADR-0010 SIM-04/05) *(Amended 2026-10-29 — ADR-0006 sync)*. GIVEN Godot 4.7.2 (the engine reference has no scripting or core file), WHEN a measured spike runs, THEN 64-bit arithmetic behaves as the formulas assume, `RefCounted` block cost is acceptable and no behaviour relies on `Dictionary` iteration order (section-keyed arrays are stored sorted). [AT-1, AT-28]
- **AC-AT-54b (auto, performance; budget provisional: the technical-director confirms) Per-run cost.** GIVEN 96 emitters, 14 loaded sections and six seats pushing, WHEN `attention.step` runs 10,000 times on each reference machine, RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (a release export, `qa.min_spec`, delegated ruling R12) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*, THEN p99 cost is at most 0.05 ms and no run exceeds `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4; the `attention.state` snapshot size stays within the bound Save/Load sets. Under `tests/performance/attention/`; pending spike measurement on RM-DESKTOP / RM-DECK. [AT-1, AT-28]

### H. Cross-system discharge

- **AC-AT-74 (review) LSG:1063 discharged.** GIVEN this GDD and the Horror Entities GDD when written, WHEN reviewed, THEN every call site of M17 RUPTURE, GOUGE and STRESS and every environmental M18 EXOGENOUS source is preceded by Attention's tell, named in AT-15, and none relies on the Graph's gate. The Attention side can be evaluated now; the Horror side is reviewable now against `horror-entities.md` (HE-17, HE-F17 gate every RUPTURE, GOUGE and bodily hit on `exogenous_permitted` at the call tick) and is implementation-blocked, not document-blocked *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [LSG:1063]
- **AC-AT-75 (review) AC-SL-121 discharged.** GIVEN this GDD, WHEN reviewed, THEN `attention.view` carries `tell_active`, `tell_remaining` and `breach_phase` with the exact names and no field is `PERCEPTION_OUT`-derived. [AT-16]
- **AC-AT-76 (review) EM AC-60 discharged.** GIVEN the registrations, WHEN reviewed, THEN Attention declares `after [graph.propagate.power, emitter.place]`, Horror (designed; implementation-blocked) declares `after` Attention and `emitter.place`, and a lit lamp counts once as light and not also in `lamps_lit_w`. [AT-1, AT-4, EM AC-60]
- **AC-AT-77 (review; Horror Entities is designed: reviewed 2026-10-27, text present) Horror must-repeats.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Reviewed clause by clause against `horror-entities.md`: no noise push (HE-36), footprints by path (a) with `HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET = MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP − STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` (HE-28), `exogenous_permitted` at the three check points, the BREACH wiring and the non-lethal presence all present. **New must-repeat from this review:** a tell that ends ABORTED by the departure void (AT-22) must withdraw Horror's TELL-time ingress like any abort. GIVEN the Horror Entities GDD when written, WHEN reviewed, THEN it repeats the predicate `exogenous_permitted`, the crew-body scope, a per-run footprint budget of at most `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP - STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`, and the wiring of its single MVP behaviour to BREACH, or it surfaces a CONFLICT; the wiring is a **BLOCKING** dependency of Attention's breach teeth (gate ruling), and the GDD also provides a non-lethal presence or stalk outside BREACH so that CALM is never known-safe. [AT-11, AT-15, C22]
- **AC-AT-78 (review; Adaptive Audio & Tells is designed: reviewed 2026-10-27, four diffs) Adaptive Audio must-repeats.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Present in `adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`: the bed (AA-16), `phase` not `breach_phase`, the dedupe key, no catch-up, the abort release (AA-19), the alarm cadence and fallback (AA-20, D.2), mix priority (AA-11, M3 verbatim), the breach-entry bound (`BREACH_ENTRY_STEP_LU_MAX`), CALM never an all-clear (AA-21), no music before beat 0 (AA-23) and the disjoint families. **Diffs found:** (1) the bed ends at BREACH entry, which this GDD's V9 got wrong and now follows; (2) M2 is per band, adopted; (3) Audio's M-4 still names a HUD glyph (cross-GDD note, C27); (4) Audio quotes `AMBIENT_CUE_FLOOR_PM` 550 and the old edges, now 800 (cross-GDD note); (5) *(user rulings)* tiers follow the escalation clock and OQ-AA-34's recommendation to reject freezing is superseded, and AA-42 admits the opt-in beat haptic *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. GIVEN the Adaptive Audio & Tells GDD when written, WHEN reviewed, THEN it repeats: the non-positional bed audible to every living player; reading `phase` not `breach_phase`; the dedupe key `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)`; no catch-up burst; the abort release cue; the alarm cadence and fallback (M1); mix priority (M3); the breach-entry loudness bound (M4); `phase == CALM` never an all-clear; no music change before beat 0; and a family disjoint from the Graph's, Horror's and Sanity's audio (M6). [AT-9, AT-12, AT-26, AUD-1 to AUD-10]
- **AC-AT-79 (review, at the confirming `/review-all-gdds` or consistency scan) Neighbour refreshes.** GIVEN the refresh list (C1 to C40 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the table runs to C40, not C25)*), WHEN the stale-token list in Cross-References is run over every refresh target, THEN it returns zero hits in each (the count and the targets scanned are recorded; a bare zero is not a result), and a diff of the neighbours shows no value changed by this GDD. [Cross-References]
- **AC-AT-80 (playtest; the Moment-ordering and breaches-per-session clauses are a first-playable gate, the rest non-blocking) The valley and fatigue.** GIVEN at least 8 recorded sessions of 25 to 35 minutes, WHEN analysed from the AT-32 counters, THEN: *abort share* (restraint aborts / (restraint aborts + breaches); voids are counted separately and excluded, AT-32 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*) is 30 to 60 percent; experienced players abort at most 75 percent of tells (this is the acceptance target; the 85 percent of E-38 is the escalation tripwire at which the freeze is judged a ritual and `ABORT_RELIEF_PM` or `REFRACT_TICKS` is pulled); fewer than 20 percent of *sorties* (the interval between leaving the home ship and returning) show 3 or more consecutive aborts; *calm time* (ticks in CALM / total ticks) is at least 20 percent over a reckless trace; and both *Moment A* (the player stops every input after the level crosses `theta` and the tell aborts) and *Moment B* (the player keeps working through a tell and a breach commits) occur in at least 6 of 8 sessions (the Moment-ordering guard, the tripwire for commit-dominance, AT-11); and *breaches per session* (told breaches in a 25-to-35-minute session) lie in the registered range 3 to 8 in at least 6 of 8 sessions *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (`BREACHES_PER_SESSION`; below 3 is throttle play, E-65; above 8 is riding every tell). Retained as a playtest report in `production/qa/evidence/`. [Player Fantasy, F-25]

- **AC-AT-134 (auto calibration + playtest; deferred: Derelict Authoring's pod-distance distribution and the AC-AT-57 harness) Void, restraint and breach per tell.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* GIVEN the reference sorties of the AC-AT-57 harness on derelicts A and B, with the pod-to-locus walking distance drawn from Derelict Authoring's authored distribution (routed to the level-designer), and three scripted policies at every derelict tell (walk to the pod; stop and set everything down; keep working), WHEN each tell is resolved, THEN the harness reports, per tell and per sortie, P(void), P(restraint abort) and P(breach) for each policy, and the share of tells for which walking out beats stopping (the hop arrives before the due run and the haul is kept), P(next tell's locus is the home ship | void) and P(a TELL opens before the voided tell's rearm ends | void) (expected 0 by AT-10) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*. The playtest half reports the same three shares from the AT-32 counters (voids separated from restraint aborts). No pass band is set here: the result is recorded against OQ-AT-36 (decided: leave it, R21) and informs the AC-AT-80 band of 3 to 8 breaches per session, the gate it feeds. NOT ASSESSED until the distribution exists. [AT-22, AT-32, E-65, Moment D]

### Gaps / untestable as written

1. **AC-AT-31** needs a named Graph read for the STRESS armed state; *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* LS-19 now names `stress_active(section_id)` (`step_no < stress_until_step`), so the test accessor is retired once the Graph owner confirms it is the published read (OQ-AT-6).
2. **Deferred integration items** *(re-evaluated 2026-10-27 — design review)*: Web Map, Horror Entities, Modular Ship Assembly, Adaptive Audio & Tells and Sanity & Hallucination Rules are all Designed now, so AC-AT-46's notification half, 68, 72, 74 and 76 (Horror halves), 77, 78 and the audio half of AC-AT-47 are no longer blocked on a missing document: 77 and 78 were reviewed (results in their text), the others wait on implementation, and AC-AT-109 still waits for Sanity's fringe asset. Under the project evidence rule an Integration story still needs an integration test or a documented playtest, so every item that cannot run yet stays a named sprint blocker and is never counted as a pass.
3. **AC-AT-54b** has no budget until the technical-director sets it (0.05 ms is the primary's proposal, not a measurement).
4. **AC-AT-21's beat-count band** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* is now pinned from the integer model (19 / 20 / 23 at jitter +8 / 0 / −8; 19 to 23 under the real hash); the qa-lead's earlier band of 19 to 22 failed the −8 case.
5. **Player Fantasy's Moment A wording** no longer carries tick arithmetic (gate legibility ruling): it says only that an abort always costs the partial *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, the price of restraint chosen on purpose. The CR-TIMELINE numerics (480 + 114 = 594 ticks, six inside `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`) live in F-15b and are pinned by AC-AT-105, so a retune shows as a diff.
6. **F-25's occupancy sentence** was corrected (22.6 percent is inside the 15 to 35 percent calm-time target and above AC-AT-80's 20 percent floor).
7. **Lint negative fixtures** are listed where the qa-lead asked for them; the data-lints without one named (AC-AT-52 and AC-AT-64 excepted) take their fixtures from the first row of Tuning Knobs whose edge is invariant-bound (a named lint, `CONTENT_INVARIANT`) or schema-bound (`CONTENT_FIELD_RANGE`), per the two classes of AC-AT-106; an out-of-range row that no invariant or schema bound binds gives only the advisory CI diff and is not a negative fixture *(Amended 2026-10-29 — sync knock-on fix)*.
8. **Not testable here by design:** the dB audibility margin of the bed (M2, a listening test owned by Adaptive Audio), beam strobing in sync with the cadence (E-20: the Controller adapter's debounce owns its criterion), and the world-side photosensitivity ceiling (the accessibility-specialist confirms).
9. **Owner, gate and status for every criterion without a mechanical pass condition yet** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. Each is recorded **NOT ASSESSED**, never passed, until its gate clears: AC-AT-54b and AC-AT-124 (technical-director; gate: the performance spike on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK, release export, delegated ruling R12: pending spike measurement; OQ-AT-33 closed) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)*; AC-AT-58 (Graph owner; gate: the integer tolerance, OQ-AT-6); AC-AT-80 and AC-AT-125 (game-designer with the producer; gate: the first playable, coded by the rubric of Feel Acceptance Criteria); AC-AT-109 (art-director; gate: Sanity's fringe asset); AC-AT-114 (art-director; gate: the ART 3.5 trail-contrast number); AC-AT-47, AC-AT-121, AC-AT-122 and AC-AT-123 (audio-director; gate: Audio's reference scene and the OQ-AA-4 latency spike); AC-AT-129's latency half (gate: the same spike); AC-AT-132's live-choice half (gate: the boarding spike, R5); AC-AT-134 (level-designer with the game-designer; gate: Derelict Authoring's pod-distance distribution and the AC-AT-57 harness); AC-AT-136 (audio-director; gate: Audio's beat assets). **Review criteria as grep-token checklists** (each run records the file, the token and the hit count; a bare zero is not a result): AC-AT-61 over this GDD: `per-ring thresholds`, `suppressant verb`, `rate-coupled`, `co-op scaling` appear only in AT-30, AC-AT-61 and Open Questions; AC-AT-74 and AC-AT-77 over `horror-entities.md`: `exogenous_permitted`, `HE-36`, `HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET`, `BREACH`, `horror.tell`; AC-AT-75 over `save-load-and-versioned-format.md`: `tell_active`, `tell_remaining`, `breach_phase`, `restore_epoch`; AC-AT-76 over `horror-entities.md` and this GDD: `after`, `attention.step`, `emitter.place`, `graph.propagate.power`; AC-AT-78 over `adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`: `breach_phase`, `tell_epoch`, `BREACH_ENTRY_STEP_LU_MAX`, `ALARM_DUTY_MAX_PM`, `RESERVED_TELL_IMPULSE_VOICES`, `void`; AC-AT-79 over each C-row target: the C-row's own tokens.

Owner: **user** = needs the user's decision, **agent** = a named specialist or neighbouring GDD owner, **GDD** = this document resolves it at a later pass. Target: playtest, next GDD, ADR, or a gate. The user-decision items come first; each carries the proposed default this GDD already follows.*
